## Document 018: 2026-10-14_［みずほ経済EXPRESS］日本の原油輸入価格は今後さらに上昇へ_5c83f50b

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026-10-14_［みずほ経済EXPRESS］日本の原油輸入価格は今後さらに上昇へ_5c83f50b.pdf
Report source: Mizuho
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

みずほ経済 EXPRESS

日本の原油輸入価格は今後さらに上昇へ 
～物価上振れリスクが強まれば、一段の利上げ加速も～

調査部 主任エコノミスト 東深澤 武史 
池田 亮平 
エコノミスト 阿部 大樹 
シニア日本経済エコノミスト 越山 祐資 
080-1069-4919  takeshi.higashifukasawa@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 日銀は物価上振れ抑制に舵。中東情勢に起因する物価押し上げリスクは予断を許さず 
9 月日銀会合後の会見で植田総裁は「政策局面が変化した」と述べた（図表 1） 。これまで日銀は、
基調的物価の押し上げを金融政策運営の重点としてきたが、足元では 2％前後の物価上昇が定着しつ
つあるとみられる。こうしたもとで、金融政策運営の重点は物価上振れの回避に移っており、そのた
めに緩和的な金融環境をなるべく早く脱することが重視される。日銀の基本的なスタンスが、連続利
上げや、0.25％の利上げ幅を上回る大幅利上げは回避するのが望ましいというものであることも踏ま
えると、今後の利上げペースは 3 カ月に一回程度、0.25％ずつが基本になるだろう 1。 
ただし、金融政策の重点が物価上振れ回避にシフトしているなかでは、よりフォワードルッキング
な政策運営が求められる 2。したがって、 原油調達コストの上昇や円安など、 物価上振れリスクの強ま
り次第で利上げペースが速まる可能性がある。先行きの物価を押し上げる主なリスク要因として、日
図表1 9月会合後の総裁会見のポイント 図表2 WTI原油先物

（出所） 日本銀行より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成 （出所） LSEGより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 2

2

銀は、原油高等の中東情勢影響、AI 関連需要の強さ、円安の三点を挙げている。このうち、原油価格
は足元で再び上昇しており、先行きの不確実性も大きい。中東情勢は、物価を取り巻く現下最大のリ
スクだろう。 
■ 不安定な情勢下で再び原油価格が上昇。タンカー運賃も急騰しダブルパンチの様相 
WTI原油先物は、9月に入って一時1バレル=100ドル台と、5月中旬以来の水準に上昇した （図表2） 。
足元では原油価格は幾分低下しているものの、水準としては依然高い状態が続いている。背景には、
中東産原油の供給懸念が強まったことがある。7月下旬以降、 サウジアラビアとイエメン・フーシ派に
よる戦闘が開始し、 ホルムズ海峡を代替する重要な原油輸送ルートとして活用されてきたバブ ・ エル ・
マンデブ海峡の通航リスクが高まった。 また、9月に入り、 サウジアラビアの原油パイプラインが攻撃
を受けて停止したと伝わると、中東産原油の域外輸送が一段と困難になるとの見方が広がった。 
中東情勢が再度悪化するなかで上昇しているのは原油価格だけではない。原油価格の上昇を大幅に
上回る勢いで、原油タンカーの運賃が急騰している（図表3）。8月以降に上昇基調が鮮明となり、9月
中旬には一時、中東情勢悪化前（2026年初）の約46倍、情勢悪化後の3～7月頃と比べても約3.2倍に達
した。 海峡航行リスクが高まるもと、 積み替えや迂回によって原油輸送の効率が悪化しているためだ。
海事データを活用して商品市場を分析しているベルギーの調査会社Kplerによると、 ホルムズ海峡の内
側にあるペルシャ湾から原油を運び出す船と、海峡の外側から最終目的地まで運ぶ船を分け、洋上で
原油を積み替える方式が広がっているようだ。 長距離輸送を担う船が危険な航路を通らずに済む反面、
積み替え作業や待機に時間を要するため、船を次の輸送に回しにくくなる。また、危険な海域を避け
るために遠回りする輸送も増え、船が一度の航海に費やす日数も長くなっている。より多くの輸送資
源が必要となったことで船舶需給がひっ迫し、タンカー運賃が大きく押し上げられている格好だ。

図表3 原油タンカーの運賃 図表4 原油輸入単価と原油価格

当たり換算収益。日本を含む東アジア向け運賃の代理指標。

前月比の伸びとして試算。中東情勢悪化を背景に、3月はドバイ原油
の価格形成機能が損なわれたと指摘されているため。原油輸入単価

■ 今後は日本の原油調達コストが上昇。企業間取引価格は反転加速を予想 
原油価格の上昇に原油タンカー運賃の急騰が重なり、先行きの日本の原油輸入単価（単位量あたり
の原油調達コスト）は一段と上昇する見込みだ3。 中東産原油が日本に輸送されるまでには1～2カ月程
度の期間を要するため、8月中旬以降の原油価格上昇 ・原油タンカー運賃上昇は10月以降の原油輸入単
価を押し上げ、その後国内の企業間取引価格へと影響が波及していくと考えられる。足元の原油価格
とタンカー運賃を踏まえると、10月の原油輸入単価は1バレル＝約140ドル程度と、中東情勢悪化後で
最も高い水準に上昇する可能性が高い（図表4）。この影響を輸入全体の平均価格（円建てベース）で
みると、10月の伸びは前年比＋29％程度と、中東情勢悪化後に最も輸入物価の伸びが加速した6・7月
に迫る勢いになる計算だ。さらに、その後は、石油化学製品などの関連財を中心に、国内の企業間取
引価格が上昇しよう。 足元で上昇圧力が一服しつつある国内企業物価は再び加速し、11月頃に同＋7％
台の伸びになると予想する（図表5）。 
加えて、原油輸入金額が膨らんで貿易収支が悪化すれば、円安圧力が強まる可能性がある。原油の
代替調達が進展した6月以降は1兆円に迫る貿易赤字が続いているが、特に10月以降は原油輸入単価が
上昇するもとで本邦貿易赤字が拡大する公算が大きく、実需面からの円安圧力が強まりやすい（図表
6） 。 足元の為替相場は、 米国の利上げ観測を背景に円安に振れやすい地合いだ。 そこに実需の動きが
重なり、1ドル＝160円を上回るようなドル高円安が進行すれば、輸入物価の上振れを通じて、国内企
業物価の伸びが一層高まるリスクがある。 
■ 物価上昇圧力が長期化する場合は利上げペース加速も視野に 
以上のように、当面の企業間取引価格に対しては押し上げ圧力が持続する公算が大きい。物価上昇
圧力が長期化するほど消費者物価への価格転嫁姿勢も強まる可能性があるだけに、日銀は物価上振れ
に対する高い警戒レベルを維持することになろう。 
仮に物価上振れリスクが大きく高まる場合には、 連続利上げ ・大幅利上げの可能性も排除できない。
中東情勢の混乱が長期化し、27年以降も原油輸入単価が一段と上振れることが見込まれる場合、ある
図表5 輸入物価（円建て）と国内企業物価 図表6 貿易収支

### Page 4

4

いは1ドル=160円を持続的に上回る水準まで円安が進展する場合には 、連続利上げや大幅利上げのシ
ナリオが現実味を帯びてくるだろう。

1 中立金利の水準は不透明であり、利上げの影響はタイムラグをともなって現れる。連続利上げ・大幅利上げによって意図せず
金融引き締めに転じれば、経済へのマイナス影響が必要以上に強まるリスクがある。 
2 中東情勢悪化以降で最初の利上げを決定した 6 月会合を振り返っても、日銀は川上の企業物価の加速を理由の一つとして利上
げに踏み切っている。6 月会合の声明文において日銀は、国内企業物価上昇率が前年比＋6.3％（5 月時点の速報ベース）に加速
したことを踏まえ、 「企業間取引におけるやや速いスピードでの価格転嫁が、消費者段階における幅広い品目の価格上昇に波及
していく可能性がある」と評価した。 
3 中東情勢悪化以降、日本の原油輸入単価は国際市況から大きく上振れている。背景として、主な代替調達先である米国産原油
が需給ひっ迫の懸念等から値上がりし、割高になったことなどが挙げられる。経済産業省発表によると、秋口以降は中東産原油
の調達が増加し、割高な米国産原油に対する依存度は低下すると見込まれるものの、本文記載のように中東産原油の輸送コスト
が大幅に高まっていることから、先行きも割高感の解消は見込みづらい。

## Document 032: 20261023【投研報告】市場快訊_AI資金壓力拖累科技類股，惟產業需求仍具韌性

Source PDF: all report pdf/20261023【投研報告】市場快訊_AI資金壓力拖累科技類股，惟產業需求仍具韌性.pdf
Report source: Yuanta (市場評論)
Date: 2026-10-23
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

AI 資金壓力拖累科技股，惟產業需求仍具韌性
2026 年 10 月 23 日
一、 市場動態
1. 費半再度重挫，科技股成為美股主要賣壓來源：美國股市主要指數 10 月 22 日跌多漲
少， 道瓊工業指數上漲0.02%，收52,867.98 點；S&P 500 指數下跌 1.21%，收7,663.43
點；Nasdaq 綜合指數下跌 2.60%，收 26,008.86 點，創 6 月 5 日以來最大單日跌幅
。費城半導體指數重挫 6.72%，收 10,731.98 點，跌幅明顯大於主要股價指數，顯示
AI 及半導體族群仍是本波市場修正的主要壓力來源。

2. AI 相關企業全面承壓 ， 資金流向非科技股及公債 ：10 月 22 日 Meta 下跌約 7%、Lam
Research 跌幅逾一成，Nvidia 下跌 4.4%，其他 AI 相關股票亦明顯走低，顯示市場
對 AI 資本支出及投資報酬的疑慮已由大型科技平台延伸至半導體與基礎建設供應商
。不過，道瓊上漲 0.02%，羅素 2000 指數亦小幅收紅，反映資金並未全面撤出股市
；美國 10 年期公債殖利率則因避險需求跌破 5%，顯示資金由高評價科技股轉向其他
產業及避險資產。
3. 中東地緣政治風險升溫，油價攀至 5 月以來高點：中東能源運輸及供給風險 10 月 22
日再度升高，布蘭特原油價格上漲約 3.76%，升至每桶 113 美元附近，創 5 月以來高
點。油價攀升除使市場重新關注能源成本對通膨與 Fed 政策路徑的影響，也可能增加
AI 資料中心的電力及營運成本。中東情勢雖非本次半導體股重挫的唯一因素，但在市
場已擔憂 AI 投資效益的情況下，能源價格上揚進一步壓抑投資人風險偏好。

二、 下跌原因分析：
1. 殖利率回落難抵科技股下跌，顯示修正因素出現轉變：10 月 14 日費半重挫主要受到
美國 9 月 CPI 高於預期、升息疑慮及美債殖利率盤中急升影響，惟當日 10 年期殖利
率收盤反而小幅下降；10 月 22 日情況則更加明顯，美國 10 年期公債殖利率因避險
需求盤中跌破 5%，費半仍大跌 6.72%。這顯示市場關注焦點已由單純的利率與估值
壓力，逐步轉向 AI 資本支出的資金來源、融資能力及投資回收期。因此，即使後續殖
利率回落，若企業仍無法證明AI 投資能轉化為足夠營收與現金流，科技股評價仍可能
持續受到壓抑。
2. Meta 自由現金流轉負 ，AI 投資成本由預期轉為實際財務壓力 ：Meta 第三季營收 646.0
億美元、年增 26%，顯示核心業務仍維持強勁成長；但資本支出達 349.1 億美元，超
過同期營業現金流 327.9 億美元，使自由現金流由去年同期正 106.3 億美元轉為負
21.2 億美元。公司亦將全年資本支出預估調整至 1,350～1,450 億美元，並宣布設立

無表決權 Class C 股票，增加未來資本運用彈性。這反映 AI 投資規模已開始超越部分

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
大型科技企業當期現金創造能力，但 Meta 仍持有 824.5 億美元現金、約當現金及有
價證券，不能將單季自由現金流轉負直接解讀為財務危機。
3. 資本稀缺逐漸成為 AI 擴張瓶頸，新興雲端業者面臨更大壓力：AI 發展需要持續投入
GPU、伺服器、資料中心及電力基礎建設，龐大資金需求使融資能力逐漸成為決定企
業成長速度的重要因素。相較具備穩定營業現金流與龐大資產規模的大型科技平台，
新興 AI 雲端服務商如 CoreWeave 及 Nebius 高度依賴外部融資，因而更容易受到借
貸成本、信用利差及投資人風險偏好變化影響。我們認為，這次修正並不代表 AI 運算

需求突然消失，而是市場開始重新評估不同企業能否負擔下一階段的擴張成本。
4. 半導體設備財報強勁卻遭賣壓，市場提高獲利兌現標準：Lam Research 於 10 月 21
日公布截至 2026 年 9 月季度（FY2027 Q1）營收 82.19 億美元，年增 54.4%、季增
22%，非 GAAP 營業利益率達 40.2%，下一季營收指引為 86 億美元、上下浮動 4 億
美元。然而，即使營運仍快速成長，10 月 22 日股價仍大幅下跌。我們研判，市場已
由過去重視 AI 訂單與營收成長，轉向同時檢視未來成長能否超越原先偏高預期，以及
AI 投資是否具備足夠的財務報酬。當股價已反映高度成長期待，即使財報表現良好，
也可能因評價調整與籌碼降溫而承壓。
5. 油價再度上漲，Fed 政策風險仍可能限制科技股評價：儘管 10 月 22 日美債殖利率因
避險需求下滑，但能源價格上漲仍可能延緩通膨降溫，使 Fed 後續政策路徑維持不確
定性。若油價持續高檔，不僅可能推升企業運輸、電力及營運成本，也將增加通膨預

期，進而限制長天期美債殖利率的下行空間。因此，科技股短線同時面臨AI 投資報酬
受到重新檢視 ， 以及能源價格與利率政策不確定的雙重壓力 。 後續10 月 28 日 FOMC
會後聲明及主席記者會，將是評估利率環境的重要依據。
三、 市場看法：
1. 股市下跌但多數個股上漲 ， 顯示賣壓集中於大型科技股 ：10 月 22 日 S&P 500 指數下
跌 1.21%，但根據美聯社（AP）報導，約三分之二成分股逆勢上漲，追蹤 S&P 500
等權重指數的 ETF（RSP）更上漲 0.73%，道瓊工業指數亦小幅收紅，呈現市值加權
指數與市場廣度明顯背離的現象 。這反映本波修正主要集中於權重較高的 AI 及大型科
技股，而非整體美股同步走弱。相較上週以防禦性類股輪動為主的情況，本週更值得
注意的是市場對高資本支出及高評價科技企業的重新定價，資金仍持續流向其他產業
，而非全面性風險撤離。只要賣壓未進一步擴散，現階段仍較接近科技股評價調整與
類股輪動，而非整體美股多頭格局反轉。
2. 台積電財測再度上修，顯示 AI 需求尚未出現結構性反轉：台積電 10 月 15 日法說會

將 2026 年美元營收成長預估由原先略高於 40%上修至約 44%， 第三季營收達新台幣
1.49 兆元、年增 50.9%，稅後純益年增 68.7%，每股盈餘 29.43 元、同樣年增 68.7%
；第四季美元營收預估為 523～543 億美元，以中間值計算季增約 14%。此外，Lam
Research 截至 9 月季度營收年增 54.4%，亦顯示半導體設備需求仍強勁。前述這些

，而非半導體終端需求同步惡化。只要大型雲端服務商未全面縮減AI 資本支出，先進
製程與設備需求中期仍具支撐。
3. AI 投資進入報酬驗證階段，營收成長能否追上資本投入成為關鍵：我們認為，AI 產業
目前正由過去的資本支出擴張階段，逐步轉向投資報酬率與自由現金流的驗證階段。
以 Meta 為例，第三季營收仍成長 26%，但資本支出已超過營業現金流，顯示即使
AI 技術有助於改善廣告推薦及營收變現，龐大基礎建設投入仍可能使短期自由現金流

承壓。不過，資料中心及運算設備通常具有多年使用年限，不能僅以單季現金流評估
整體投資成敗。真正關鍵在於未來 AI 服務收入、營業利益及現金流成長，能否逐步抵
銷前期資本投入。具備明確商業化模式、較高資本效率及穩定現金流的企業，後續表
現可望優於高度依賴融資與成長預期的業者。
4. AI 融資風險仍集中於部分企業 ， 尚不足以認定信用危機全面擴散 ：Meta Q3 財報顯示
，該公司持有 824.5 億美元現金、約當現金及有價證券，顯示其具備一定財務緩衝能
力；相較之下，新興AI 雲端業者因資料中心建置成本龐大、現金流尚未成熟，對信用
市場與外部融資條件的敏感度相對較高 。雖然 AI 資本支出引發投資人重新評估企業信
用風險，但我們認為目前仍缺乏足夠證據認定風險已擴散至整體投資級債市場或銀行
體系。然而，後續仍宜密切觀察 AI 相關企業 CDS 與公司債利差變化、再融資能力及
主要金融機構授信條件對於科技類股及整體股債市場的後續影響。
5. 股價修正不代表企業獲利同步下修，財報表現仍有機會支撐科技股：儘管近期半導體

及 AI 相關股價回落，但台積電與Lam Research 最新財報顯示，部分供應鏈企業營收
及獲利仍維持高度成長，反映股票價格與企業基本面短期可能出現明顯落差。當市場
因融資及資本支出疑慮而降低評價倍數時，即使企業獲利持續增加，股價也可能暫時
承壓。然而，若後續大型科技公司公布的雲端營收、AI 訂單與獲利展望持續優於預期
，企業 EPS 上修仍有機會抵銷部分本益比壓縮壓力。因此，目前尚不宜將科技股價格
修正直接等同於 AI 產業景氣反轉，企業獲利能否持續兌現，仍是中期市場方向的重要
支撐。

6. Fed 與大型科技股財報進入驗證期，短線震盪可能延續：Fed 將於 10 月 27～28 日舉
行利率決策會議，市場除關注是否調整政策利率外，更應重視會後聲明及主席記者會
對通膨、油價與後續利率路徑的態度。另一方面，大型雲端服務商後續公布的財報，
將提供 AI 資本支出、雲端營收及投資效率的最新證據。短線科技股仍可能受到市場評
價偏高、融資風險及地緣政治因素影響而維持震盪；不過，只要企業獲利成長與 AI
需求未出現結構性反轉，中期科技產業基本面仍具支撐，現階段維持短線審慎、中期
偏正向的看法。
四、市場展望：

1. 科技股短線宜審慎觀察 ， 中期仍看好具基本面支撐的AI 產業 ：近期科技股受到 AI 資本支
出、融資能力與評價調整等因素影響，短線波動可能延續，尤其高度依賴外部資金及獲利
尚未成熟的 AI 基礎建設業者，仍面臨較大不確定性。然而，台積電及 Lam Research 最
新財報顯示，先進製程與半導體設備需求尚未出現全面轉弱。我們認為，科技股中期展望
仍偏正向，但市場將更重視企業營收成長、訂單能見度、自由現金流及資本效率。相較缺
乏穩定獲利的 AI 概念企業，具技術優勢、財務結構穩健及獲利成長能見度的科技企業，
風險報酬相對有利。
2. AI 產業由全面成長轉向基本面分化，現金流將成為選股關鍵：隨著大型科技企業持續擴
大 AI 基礎建設投資，市場對未來投資報酬率的要求將同步提高。就目前已公布的科技股
財報結果來看，AI 產業長期成長趨勢尚未改變，但後續股價表現將更加分化，具備穩定
現金流、明確訂單及商業化能力的企業，相對有利；反之，對於高度依賴外部融資、自由
現金流持續為負且缺乏明確獲利時程的企業，仍宜維持審慎。

3. 美債避險功能重現，中天期債券風險報酬相對均衡：半導體類股 10/22 重挫，美國公債
價格上揚、10 年期殖利率盤中跌破 5%，顯示股市風險偏好下降時，美債仍可提供一定
避險效果。雖然 Fed 政策路徑及能源價格仍存在不確定性，但預期相較受政策利率影響
較大的短天期公債，以及受財政赤字、國債供給與期限溢價影響的超長天期公債，中天期
美國公債的風險報酬仍相對較為均衡 。 投資級債因企業信用基本面整體仍具韌性 ， 中期維
持偏正向看法；高收益債則須留意 AI 相關發行人信用風險上升及利差擴大的影響。

Summary: We attended the 2026 Taiwan Printed Circuit Association (TPCA) Show that 
was held on 20-22 October. Below, we share our read-throughs from booth visits and key 
company takeaways. Overall, we remain positive on Taiwan glass fibre, CCL, PCB, and 
substrate vendors, supported by upbeat company commentary. On pricing, we expect 
low-end CCL and E-glass price increases to be larger and more sustained than previously 
anticipated. High-end CCL vendor commentary suggests plans for more meaningful price 
increases from 2H26 to maintain their products’ relative profitability amid sharp increases 
in low-end CCL prices. We also expect prices for T-glass, ABF, CCL, and substrates to 
continue rising. On the CCL technology roadmap, we favour Q-glass combined with 
hydrocarbon resin (HC) over PTFE-based alternatives as the longer-term direction. Lastly, 
we provide our view on the CCL market competition landscape change after seeing 
Nan Ya Plastics targeting the high-end market more aggressively. We also provide some of 
our key highlights from related companies within the Taiwan PCB supply chain. EMC (2383 
TT, TWD4,750, Buy [1]), ITEQ (6213 TT, TWD746, Buy [1]), Kinsus (3189 TT, TWD941, 
Buy [1]) and NYPCB (8046 TT, TWD1,255, Buy [1]) are our top ideas within our coverage.

We attended the 2026 Taiwan Printed Circuit Association (TPCA) Show last week. Below, 
we share our read-throughs from booth visits and key company takeaways.

Whether to adopt PTFE emerged as a key point of debate at the show.  
• During the TPCA show, Taiwan Union Technology (TUC; 6274 TT, TWD1,280, Buy [1]) 
unveiled its new M10 (Hydrocarbon base) and M11 (PTFE base) materials with glass-
fabric (Low-Dk2, Q-glass) and glass-fabric-free versions. Nan Ya Plastic (1303 TT, not 
rated) also unveiled its M11-grade material (ie, NPG-A99) at the show. Based on our 
understanding, NPG-A99 will continue to a hydrocarbon resin (HC) base, likely 
combined with Low-Dk2, to enhance CCL performance for high frequency and high-
speed signal transmission requirements. However, Elite Material (EMC; 2383 TT, 
TWD4,750, Buy [1]) remains reluctant to enter the PTFE-based CCL market, citing 
limited end application. Also, EMC plans to use advanced Q-glass for its M10 CCL. 
Lastly, we also heard from Panasonic’s booth representative that Panasonic (6752 JP, 
JPY3,687, Neutral [3]; covered by Ayaka Inomata) is pursuing a similar strategy for 
M9/M10 development, leveraging Q-glass instead of PTFE at this stage.

• While Nvidia (NVDA US, USD216, Outperform [2]; covered by Louis Miscioscia) 
appears keen to enhance product performance by adopting PTFE, given its very low 
dissipation factor (Df), PCB manufacturing yields remain a key concern. Accordingly, 
some leading vendors are broadening their material options (including PTFE-based 
CCL) to address potential customer requirements. EMC and Panasonic, however, 
remain firmly committed to a Q-glass-based technology roadmap. Although TUC 
highlighted progress on its PTFE-based M11 at a technology conference during the 
show, we believe it also intends to advance its existing HC resin/Low-Dk2 and HC 
resin/Q-glass roadmap for its next-generation CCL. In our view, its PTFE-based M11 
development is intended to preserve flexibility across potential technology pathways 
rather than replace its existing approach. Nan Ya Plastics’s strategy also appears 
reasonable: it can produce Low-Dk glass in-house but must source Q-glass and PTFE 
externally. This sourcing structure may explain its continued use of Low-Dk2 with HC-
resin instead of PTFE for its next-gen products, including M11.

• Conclusion: which technology roadmap will prevail? We believe it is too early to 
identify a clear winner, as the supply chain continues to improve manufacturing yields 
for PTFE-based products. Some vendors remain cautious despite PTFE’s long history in 
the industry, as its thermal shrinkage and relative softness still make adoption in high-
layer-count (HLC) PCBs challenging. Given the current stance of leading global vendors 
EMC, Doosan (000150 KS), and Panasonic, together with EMC’s execution track 
record, we currently favour Q-glass-based solutions without PTFE as the more likely 
direction. Nevertheless, broadening product offerings to address different customer 
requirements should not be viewed negatively, provided vendors have sufficient 
resources to support development across multiple technology pathways.

Low-end CCL and e-glass continue to surprise on the upside.  
• During the show, we also spoke with Kingboard Laminates (1888 HK, not rated). The 
company remained positive on the E-glass outlook, although its booth representative 
provided no numerical guidance for next year following a c.200% price hike in 2026. 
Kingboard attributed the pricing strength to supply constraints, driven largely by AI 
server demand. While we highlighted increased glass yarn supply from Chinese peers in 
2Q/3Q26, the company argued that the weaving machine capacity remains the 
bottleneck. It expects to add only c.500 weaving machines in 2026 and 1.2k in 2027 (vs. 
current installed base of 3.3k). E-glass fabric price increases therefore appear 
supported by limited weaving capacity and producers shifting capacity from lower-end 
glass to higher-end products, including Low-Dk, Low-Dk2, and T-glass. Kingboard also 
noted a similar trend in low-end copper foil, where shortages have recently intensified.

• The read-through from Nan Ya Plastics is similar. The company indicated that its current 
fibreglass allocation between internal consumption and external sales is 45:55, with the 
internal share set to rise gradually as it upgrades its product mix. Its CCL portfolio 
remains concentrated in FR-4 (M2 grade), but it plans to migrate toward M4/M6, 
supported by long-term contract wins with two major hyperscalers seeking to secure 
CCL supply. In 2027, M4/M6 products are expected to account for 60-70% of its mix (4m 
out of 6.5m in capacity), with M2 and below contributing 20%. The remainder would 
comprise high-end CCL, including M7/M8/M9, reflecting recent development progress. 
We therefore expect Nan Ya’s external thin-type E-glass supply to decline as its M4/M6 
mix increases next year.

Competition from Nan Ya Plastics.   
• The current CCL shortage creates favourable conditions for Nan Ya Plastics to expand 
into the high-end CCL supply chain. Whether the company has entered AMZN’s T3/T4 
supply chain is also a key point of market debate. Based on discussions with its booth 
representatives, however, we believe management will prioritise upgrading its M4/M6 
product portfolio before pursuing the highest-end CCL market more aggressively. On the 
positive side, favourable supply-demand dynamics have supported Nan Ya’s transition 
from a low-end supplier toward the mid-to-high-end segment, although high-end CCL 
should still contribute a relatively small share of revenue in 2027. For incumbent 
suppliers, competition is clearly intensifying, but we expect the near-term impact on 
high-end peers to remain limited.

• Our read: We expect the impact on EMC and TUC to remain relatively limited in 2027. 
Competitive pressure could become more meaningful over 2028-2030, although it is too 
early to assess its magnitude.

Taiwan Data Centre Hardware

3

Other key takeaways: 
• Nitto Boseki (3110 JP, JPY2,942, Neutral [3]; covered by Noritsugu Hirakawa) identified 
Taiwan Glass (1802 TT, NR) and Grace Fabric Technology (603256 CH, CNY84.95, 
Outperform [2]; covered by Dennis Ip) as key competitors. Its plan to triple capacity by 
2028 remains largely on track. The company also intends to expand capacity further by 
2030, although the details have yet to be confirmed.

• Resonac (4004 JP, JPY12,915, Outperform [2]; covered by Hidemitsu Umebayashi) 
indicated that customers are requesting ABF substrate core thicknesses of 2,000-
3,000μm, versus its current maximum of 1,600μm, to accommodate larger package 
sizes. Potential technology pathways include adopting glass cores or ceramic 
substrates, or stacking additional core layers to enhance rigidity. Based on discussions 
with booth representatives, substrate makers may move toward multi-core structures, 
which could increase T-glass consumption. However, technical challenges in enabling 
signal transmission between core layers remain a potential barrier that would need to be 
addressed over the next one to two years.

• Mitsui Kinzoku (5706 JP, JPY2,054, Outperform [2]; covered by Shinichiro Ozaki) 
indicated that, based on its technology roadmap, M10-grade CCL combined with its 
HVLP5 copper foil should support 448Gbps SerDes transmission. In the near term, the 
company plans to shift capacity from lower-end to higher-end HVLP products, with a key 
piece of production equipment scheduled for installation in 2028/29. On pricing, its booth 
representative suggested that quarterly increases of more than 10% would be 
appropriate. Regarding copper foil shortages highlighted by other supply-chain 
participants, the representative indicated that mid-tier products, such as HVLP1 and 
HVLP2, could face the most severe supply constraints.

• Unimicron (3037 TT, TWD1,060, Buy [1]) appeared to highlight multi-core structures and 
the embedding of passive components within core layers as key technology trends in 
ABF substrates.

• Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT; 4958 TT, not rated) indicated that its substrate business 
has a plan to list on the Hong Kong Stock Exchange in 1H27 under the name Leading 
Interconnect Semiconductor Technology (LIST). At the show, the company focused 
primarily on thick HDI boards and highlighted two next-generation technology trends: 1) 
sequential lamination (M+N) to improve signal integrity and increase routing density 
within the same footprint; and 2) hybrid HDI/HLC structures, which add HDI layers to 
thick HLC boards (M+N), as discussed earlier.

## Document 133: 261029_daiwa_ASE

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: ASE disclosed a strong set of quarterly results and guidance 
on 29 October, both beating market expectations, thanks to the strong AI 
scaling which is just getting stronger to benefit ASE’s leading-edge 
advanced packaging (LEAP) business. We raise our forecasts and 12M TP 
and reaffirm our Outperform (2) call on the stock; accumulate into any 
technical pullback.

What’s the impact: Results and guidance all beat. ASE reported 3Q26 
net profit of c.TWD27bn, beating our/consensus estimates by 25%/18% 
thanks to higher semiconductor assembly & test (SAT, or IC ATM by 
company definition) revenue which came in at TWD151bn (USD4.7bn), 7% 
above our estimate, for higher gross margin (GM) of 30.5%, 1.9pp above 
ours and topping ASE’s structural target of 24-30%. The strength was 
amplified further by lower non-operating losses, resulting in the strong 
bottom-line beat (table on p.2). Guidance was another beat thanks to the 
strong SAT business where ASE expects revenue to grow 11-13% QoQ for 
GM to top its record of 30.5% during the COVID upturn. We raise our 2026-
28E EPS by 14-24% assuming its SAT GM to reach 33% through 2027.

Strong AI gets stronger. Discussions centred on ASE’s LEAP business, 
where management further hiked revenue target this year beyond USD4bn 
(USD1.6bn in 2025), after 3 hikes YTD, thanks to strong demand for its on-
substrate (oS) packaging and initial ramp of its full-process solutions. With 
addition of new co-packaged optics (CPO) solutions, ASE expects the 
LEAP revenue to double YoY in 2027. As LEAP is margin-accretive, ASE 
expects the SAT GM to continue improving beyond 4Q26, making the 
ceiling of its structural GM target now as the floor to kick off 2027 for a 
strong start. Given management’s emphasis on “friendly pricing”, we expect 
likely continued cost passthrough across the supply chain to add to GM 
upside. Working hard to narrow the supply/demand gap, ASE further raised 
this year’s capex to USD11.5bn (from USD10.5bn), reinforcing our view 
that AI is scaling strongly to accelerate the chip industry growth.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we raise our 12M TP 
for ASE to TWD710 (previous: TWD550), based on our revised ROE-
adjusted PBR target of 5.8x (previous: 5.2x) 2027E BVPS due to a higher 
ROE trajectory. Our revised TP translates into 24x 4-quarter forward PER, 
which we see as justifying the rich PBR target given its 2026-28E EPS 
CAGR of 25%. We reiterate our Outperform (2) call with key downside risks 
of any “silicon inflation” and geopolitical overhang leading to AI capex cut.

How we differ: Our 2026-27E EPS are 5-10% above the consensus as we 
believe the street has yet to factor in its stronger LEAP business.

29 October 2026

Entering an even stronger earnings cycle (III)

➢ 3Q26 results and 4Q26 guidance all beat expectations 
➢ Strong AI scaling is getting even stronger to benefit LEAP 
➢ Reaffirm Outperform (2) call with a higher 12M TP of TWD710

Company profile 
Established in April 2018, ASE Industrial Holding Co (HoldCo) is a holding company which privatized 
ASE and SPIL fully on 30 April 2018 through equity and cash swap, respectively, and listed on the same 
day with senior management assigned mainly by ASE. HoldCo has become the strong No.1 OSAT 
maker in the world, offering a comprehensive product portfolio to its customers from wirebonding, flip-
chip & bumping, system-in-package (SiP) to broad-based electronics manufacturing services (EMS).

### Page 5

5

ASE Technology Holding (3711 TT): 29 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 5 Feb 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
ASE’s Board of Directors consists of nine members with diverse professional backgrounds.
Three of the directors are independent, representing 33 percent of the board. The Chairman also
serves as the Chief Executive Officer, resulting in unified leadership and centralized decision-
making. To maintain effective governance and oversight under this structure, ASE relies on its
independent directors and established board processes. The board currently includes one
female director, and the company continues to pursue long-term goals related to board diversity
and balanced representation. ASE has an Audit Committee, Compensation Committee, Risk
Management Committee, and Chief Government Officer to conduct effective supervision.
Capital management 3
In the past 5 years, ASE paid over 50% or nearly 50% of its earnings in dividends. However, the
dividend trend was volatile in dollar and payout ratio terms. We forecast its dividend payout ratio
to average over 50% in the next 2 years.
Related party &
transaction 2
ASE undertakes related-party transactions with its subsidiaries on sales and purchases. The
total related-party transaction sales/purchase amounts were less than 10% of its total
sales/purchases, and the transaction terms with related parties were normal compared with
those with other customers/suppliers. Thus, we do not think related-party transactions have a
meaningful impact on overall operations.
S Supply chainmanagement 2
The supply of raw materials has the most direct impact on ASE's operations and manufacturing.
To lower overall supply-chain risks, ASE has expanded the scope of sustainability risk
management to non-tier-1 suppliers. ASE currently has over 600 non-tier-1 suppliers.
ASE requires its suppliers to comply with its Supplier Code of Conduct, which aims to ensure
that suppliers conduct business in a manner that meets labour, health & safety, environment,
business ethics & management, and various corporate compliance standards.
E Water & wastewatermanagement 2
ASE has adopted 3 water-use strategies of reduce, reuse and recycle. Through its water
management measures, it reduced water consumption density (water consumption/revenue) by
43% in 2024 vs. that of 2015. ASE is committed to increasing water reuse at its facilities in order
to improve water efficiency.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
ASE is committed to improving waste recycling and reuse through the 5 key elements of a
circular economy of: direct recycling, reuse, off-site regeneration, renewable materials, and
supply and rental. The recycling rate of general waste was 90% in 2024 and its hazardous waste
density declined by 53% in 2024 vs. that of 2015.
E GHG emissions 1
ASE is committed to achieving net zero emissions by advancing low carbon energy adoption,
developing smart grid technologies, and strengthening high tech energy management systems.
As of 2024, 19% of total manufacturing energy consumption comes from renewable sources,
88% of ASE facilities use renewable energy, and 10 sites have achieved RE100 certification.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 1

MediaTek

TPU wins on track for steep ramp through 
’28 joined by further CSP/AI lab potential

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 6,200 TWD | Price: 3,915 TWD

3Q26 beat on consumer builds; 4Q26 lifted by TPU ramp 
3Q26 sales were +10% QoQ on its flagship mobile SoC launch and customers’ pre-builds 
ahead of its chipset price hike on rising manufacturing cost. On higher sales and better 
mix, GM/OpM edged up to 46%/16%, taking 3Q26 EPS to NT$17.5 above BofAe/street. 
4Q26 sales was guided +25-32% QoQ, in line with BofA preview, as steep TPUv8t ramp 
now tracking to US$2.5bn (vs. US$2bn guide before) partially balanced by soft demand 
elsewhere (-15-22% QoQ vs. 5-year seasonal +1%) following the early pull-in. Despite 
TPUv8t growing to ~35% of its sales at lower margins, GMs is guided resiliently at 45% 
in 4Q26, implying better margins for its merchant business at 47%. Fast sales scale-up 
and stable GMs should lift operating leverage with OpMs guided for 19% at midpoint. 
ASIC pipeline spanning across CSPs and frontier AI labs 
Mediatek sees TPUv8t ramping to US$16bn+ revenue in ’27, lifting OpM to 20%+, with 
upside if it could secure more capacity. The follow on TPUv9 is tracking for a meaningful 
production potential in ’28. Importantly, it confirmed a 2nd ASIC customer win in design 
now and production contribution set to kick off in ’28. We believe this new project would 
require Mediatek’s component sourcing and supply chain management at a much higher 
dollar content. This along with potential Bytedance win could add upside to our base 
case for its data center revenue of US$19bn/US$53bn in '27/'28 with xAI ASIC revenue 
joined from 2H28 to supplement Google TPU upgrades.  
AI project diversification supports earnings and valuation 
As ASIC business inflects, we lift our 2026 EPS to NT$74.13 and keep ‘27/28 EPS at 
NT$168.93/404.32. Keep our Buy rating and NT$6,200 PO on 24x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(implied 30x ASIC EPS + 10x merchant EPS), backed by sales/EPS 90%/134% CAGR. 
Growing AI project wins should alleviate the concerns on customer concentration and 
TPU competition debate with share downside protected by 23x/10x ‘27/28 straight P/E.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
MediaTek is a fabless IC design company supplying to 
wireless communications and digital consumer markets. 
Before taking leadership in mobile SoC area, it had built 
solid ground in the TV SoC and optical disk drive markets. 
The firm invests heavily in R&D to seek better positioning 
in the 5G cycle, and has a complete offering including ASIC, 
Wi-Fi, PMIC etc. to address applications beyond 
smartphone, such as IoT and auto.

Investment Rationale 
We have Buy rating on MediaTek as we think MediaTek is 
ready and has the strength to brace for the upcoming 
proliferation of IoT devices and opportunities in growing 
markets like ASIC, ARM-based PC, auto, and etc., and we 
believe its solid balance sheet and cashflow could provide 
downside support to the share price. Meanwhile, we expect 
5G smartphone penetration to be a multi-year theme for 
the company, with its better competitive landscape in 
duopoly 5G era and an improved margin profile.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
MediaTek (MDTKF) 
Our PO of NT$6,200 is based on 24x 2H27-1H28 P/E. which is towards the higher end 
of its historical P/E range of 8-25x, as we believe the company's broadening ASIC 
pipeline could drive an earnings upgrade cycle.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) better than expected smartphone demand, especially in 
China, (2) a better pricing in mobile communication segment thanks to alleviated 
competition and rising exposure to high-end/flagship market, and (3) an even more 
superior investment return on non-op side to uplift its net cash position. (4) Unexpected 
collaboration with global technology leaders leads to higher than expected 
margins/profits

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) weaker-than-expected end-market situation in 
consumer-related smartphone/TV space, (2) macro challenges including inflation/policy-
related pressures, (3) rising competition from existing players and/or newcomers in 
smartphone/TV SoC (system on chip) space, and (4) ASP/margin pressure caused by each 
or all of the above and/or rising foundry costs. (4) Bidding process for Meta Inference 
ASIC is slower than expected.

FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating.  VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation). 
Investment rating Total return expectation (within 12-month period of date of initial 
rating)

INCOME RATINGS, indicators of potential cash dividends, are: 7 - same/higher (dividend considered to be secure), 8 - same/lower (dividend not considered to be 
secure) and 9 - pays no cash dividend.  Coverage Cluster is comprised of stocks covered by a single analyst or two or more analysts sharing a c ommon industry, 
sector, region or other classification(s). A stock’ s coverage cluster is included in the most recent BofA Global Research report referencing the stock.

### Page 2

New capacity in expansion: The company continues to expand its production scale to 
fulfill rising clients’ orders from LEO satellite and innovative business. We expect to see 
growing LEO satellite revenues contribution from 75% in 2025 to 97% in 2028E. 
Management notes the company has started the planning of next-stage expansion, and 
will start to expand capacity in Taiwan, Vietnam and US sites from 4Q26E and onwards 
to diversify the global production sites, improve the product delivery capabilities, and 
supply elasticity.

Earnings revision: We factor in UMT 3Q26 earnings and revise down earnings by 5%/ 
10%/ 9% in 2026-28E mainly on lower GM in 2026-28E by 3.8ppts~3.9ppts, reflecting 
longer time of transition to new LEO satellites platform, while we expect to see 
continuous GM improvement ahead on rising LEO satellite buisness carrying higher GM.

Valuation: We continue to derive our target price based on a discounted P/E, and based 
on 2029E (methodology unchanged). We continue to derive the target P/E multiple from 
the average PEG&M ratio of peers in the satellite supply chain, and we remove outliers 
(telecom operators who face competition from LEO satellite operators). Our target 
2029E discounted P/E multiple is at 35.1x (vs. previously at 37.1x) which is based on 
peers’ avg. PEG&M, and UMT’s 2029-30E avg. NI YoY and OPM, due to lower avg. OPM in 
2029-30E at 38% (vs. previously at 42%). We apply the 35.1x multiple to 2029E EPS and

### Page 4

Price target risks and methodology - UMT 
Valuation: We use a discounted P/E methodology and apply a 35.1x target P/E multiple 
to UMT’s 2029E EPS, discounting it back to 2027E at a COE of 7.8% (beta 1.2x, risk-free 
rate 1.6% and market risk premium at 5.1%), which leads to our 12-month target price 
of NT$2,188. The target P/E is based on the average PEG&M ratio of peers in the 
satellite supply chain. We are Buy-rated on UMT.  
Key Risks: Slower-than-expected LEO satellite deployment; Potential competition from 
new-entrant suppliers; LEO satellite operators manufacturing in-house components.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 30 Oct 2026 close.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this

欣銓 (3264 TT)  Buy

晶圓大廠新訂單逐步向上，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ：欣銓 2026 年資本支出轉向積極擴張，主要來自與策
略客戶 (晶圓代工廠 )已確定之 ASIC 合作案，其龍潭新廠無塵室
與基礎設施建置，以及子公司全智科配合美系客戶擴產。 1Q26
至 3Q26 期間取得不動產、廠房及設備 (資本支出 )累計達 102.74
億元，相較於 3Q25 累計之 27.96 億元大幅增加 74.78 億元
(YoY+267.45%) 。3Q26 已進入量產，下一階段擴產將自 2Q27 啟
動，規劃自 2Q27 起每季開出一層全新無塵室加入量產，後續樓
層無塵室擴建將以現有主要客戶需求為優先配合對象。預估欣
銓 2027 年稅後 EPS 13.20 元。龍潭廠新專案是 2026~2027 年最
重要的增長引擎。而旗下全智湖口新廠，也於 3Q26 正式進入矽
光子相關新產品的量產階段，都將帶動公司中長期營收獲利向
上，因此建議欣銓 Buy ，目標價 322 元。  
3Q26 稅後 EPS 3.01 元：龍潭新廠歷經 1H26 客戶驗證與進場裝
機後，於 07/2026 起正式進入量產階段，帶動欣銓營收成長，
3Q26 營收為 53.37 億元， QoQ+17.72% 。營收依應用別分析，通
訊 Communication/Connectivity 佔 41.0% ；車用 / 安控
(ATV/Security) 佔 17.9% ；射頻晶片 (RF IC) 佔 10.9% ；微處理器
(General MCU) 佔 8.7%；Storage 佔 9.5%；PC/Consumers 佔 6.6%；
Memory 佔 3.7% ；其他 1.8% 。毛利率的部份， 3Q26 毛利率
45.43% ，增加 5.27 個百分點。 3Q26 稅後 EPS 3.01 元。  
預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 3.03 元：受到 3Q26 營收基期墊高、歐美
客戶於 11/2026 至 12/2026 進入假期拉貨淡季 ， 以及手機與通訊
終端產品季節性調節，營收僅小幅成長，毛利率展望方面，由
於 4Q26 未有新無塵室開出，且公司需提前為 2027 年擴產招募
人力、提列初期整備成本與設備折舊增加，預期 4Q26 毛利率將
略低於 3Q26 之 45.43% ，但仍可穩健維持高檔水準；公司努力
進行成本管控和改善產品組合 ， 讓 4Q26 獲利有機會高於 3Q26。
預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 3.03 元。 2026 年稅後 EPS 10.28 元。

2 群益投顧 
  欣銓 ESG ：  
欣銓積極推動  ESG( 環境、社會、公司治理 )相關政策與行動，並於官方網站
設有專屬  ESG 頁面與歷年永續報告書，公開揭露永續發展成果與目標。  
環境 (Environmental) 
•持續推動再生能源與減碳：延續加入  RE100 的承諾，積極推動節能減碳
與再生能源採購，並簽署 SBTi( 科學基礎減碳目標倡議 )承諾，強化減碳目標
與國際接軌。  
•循環經濟新提案：攜手供應鏈夥伴推動循環箱再進化，落實資源再利用與
減廢。  
•生物多樣性與生態尊重：持續舉辦淨灘、認養友善田等活動，強調尊重生
態、友善健康。  
社會 (Social) 
•同仁參與與文化深化：舉辦「 ESG Award 」專案競賽，鼓勵員工提出永續點
子與專案，涵蓋綠色製造、責任供應鏈、多元包容職場、人才培育、關懷弱
勢等五大方向。  
• 員工關懷與職場環境：持續改善員工宿舍環境，提升同仁生活品質。  
•社會公益：贊助在地傳統藝文活動、捐助弱勢團體，並與產業園區合作舉
辦移工籃球賽，促進多元共融。  
公司治理 (Governance) 
•資訊透明與永續報告：發行永續報告書，持續自主揭露非財務資訊，強調
資訊透明與溝通成效。  
•國際認證與標準：完成 RBA VAP 8.0 改版訓練，強化人權、環保等國際標
準合規與超越法規要求。  
•永續獎項肯定：榮獲「英國標準協會 (BSI)ESG 永續發展領航獎」及「台灣
企業永續獎 (TCSA) －永續報告獎金獎」 ，肯定公司在永續發展、資訊揭露與
社會責任的表現。  
攜手合作夥伴擴大永續影響力，推動以大帶小、標竿學習。展現穩健落實永
續目標與全球標準接軌的實力。  
◼CDP 氣候問卷榮獲  A-List 最高肯定 ： 公司在2025 年  CDP 全球環境評
比中，從全球逾  22,100 家企業脫穎而出，「氣候變遷」問卷榮獲 A 級領
導等級，「水安全」蟬聯  A- 領導等級肯定。  
◼ SBTi 近程目標通過驗證：公司設定的近程減碳目標已於 04/2026 正式通
過科學基礎減量目標倡議 (SBTi) 官方驗證。  
◼連續  9 年榮獲「公司治理評鑑前  5% 」的殊榮。  
◼龍潭廠榮獲「 2026 台灣建築永續獎金獎」肯定。  
欣銓 3Q26 營收 53.37 億元， QoQ+17.72% ，稅前淨利 18.25 億元，
QoQ+25.16% ，稅後 EPS 3.01 元：

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 28 日

3 群益投顧 
由於全球  AI 需求極其暢旺，許多一線封測同業將產能高度集中在  AI 核
心晶片，導致其他「 AI 周邊晶片」或「非  AI 通訊晶片」的測試產能出現
缺口。欣銓因此獲得大量的外溢訂單 (轉單 )，特別是在通訊與 PC Consumer 
領域。欣銓 3Q26 營收為 53.37 億元， QoQ+17.72% 。  
營收依應用別分析，通訊 Communication/Connectivity 佔 41.0% ；車用 /安控
(ATV/Security) 佔 17.9%； 射頻晶片(RF IC) 佔 10.9%； 微處理器(General MCU)
佔 8.7% ； Storage 佔 9.5% ； PC/Consumers 佔 6.6% ； Memory 佔 3.7% ；其他
1.8% 。  
3Q26 終端應用結構，通訊與連網 (Communication/Connectivity) 為最大成長
引擎，營收佔比自 2Q26 的 33.4% 大幅擴張至 41.0% 。這部份含光通訊和晶
圓代工廠的訂單 。 車用與安控(ATV/Security) 營收佔比為 17.9%，略 低 於2Q26
的 18.2% ，不過營收金額是增加，車用市場回溫，營收持續向上。射頻晶片
(RF IC) 營收佔比為 10.9% ，低於 2Q26 的 12.2% ，因為手機拉貨動能下滑。
儲存裝置 (Storage) 營收佔比自 2Q26 的 10.8% 收斂至 9.5% ，因晶圓代工廠的
產能受限。通用型微控制器 (General MCU) 佔比為 8.7% ，相較 2Q26 之 8.5%
微幅增加。個人電腦與消費性晶片 (PC/Consumers) 佔比為 6.6% ，相較 2Q26
之 9.9% 明顯下降，需求受到終端價格上漲影響，加上客戶產品組合上也會
優先供應通訊產品，營收佔比下滑較明顯。記憶體 (Memory) 營收佔比為
3.7% ，低於 2Q26 的 5.4% 。其他類別佔比則由 1.60% 微升至 1.80% 。  
毛利率的部份，得益於通訊連網晶片拉貨動能強勁以及整體稼動率維持高
檔約 80%。營業毛利達 24.25 億元， QoQ+33.1% ， YoY+89.1% ，毛利率攀升
至 45.43% ，較 2Q26 增加 5.27 個百分點，高稼動率帶動規模經濟發酵。營
業利益達 18.6 億元， QoQ+35.0% ，營益率達 34.8% ，較 2Q26 成長 4.4 個百
分點，較 3Q25 大幅增加 10.5 個百分點。業外收支部分， 3Q26 業外淨支出
為 0.34 億元，稅前淨利 18.25 億元， QoQ+25.16% ，稅後 EPS 3.01 元。創下
單季獲利新高紀錄。  
累計前三季 (1Q26 至 3Q26) 合併營收達 138.54 億元， YoY+35.4% ，毛利率達
41.7% ，營業利益達 43.34 億元， YoY+69.60% ，營益率達 31.3% ，累計稅後
淨利為 35.54 億元， YoY+78.4% ，前三季累計 EPS 達 7.49 元。  
就客戶類型分析 ，1Q26 至 3Q26 累計營收中 ， 無晶圓廠IC 設計業者 (Fabless)
貢獻 52.5% ，垂直整合製造商 (IDM) 佔 42.1% ，晶圓代工廠 (Foundry) 佔比由
前三季累計的 1.8% 提高至 5.4%，其他客戶佔比為 0.1%。在產品測試型態方
面，前三季晶圓測試 (Wafer Sorting) 營收佔比達 73.2% ，成品測試 (Final Test)
佔比為 26.1% ，其他項目佔 0.7% 。  
基於與策略客戶已談定的  ASIC 合作案，預估欣銓 2026 年資本支出將明
顯增加至約 130 億元， 2027 年將會高於 2026 年：  
欣銓對資本支出一向保持保守與謹慎，主因是高額折舊若遇上稼動率下滑，
會嚴重衝擊毛利率。此次  2026 年轉向積極擴張，主要來自與策略客戶已確
定的  ASIC 合作案。欣銓 2024 至 2025 年的資本支出主要集中在龍潭新廠
的廠房興建與土建尾款； 2026 年則轉向以「機台設備」為主，廠房支出比
例將顯著降低。先進測試機台交期長達 6 - 8 個月。目前測試機台約 1,700
台以上。  
欣銓在 1Q26 至 3Q26 期間取得不動產 、 廠房及設備(資本支出 )累計達 102.74

### Page 4

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 28 日

4 群益投顧 
億元，相較於 3Q25 累計之 27.96 億元大幅增加 74.78 億元， YoY+267.45% ，
資本支出顯著放量主要由兩大核心動能驅動：第一為龍潭新廠無塵室與基
礎設施建置，第二為子公司全智科技為配合美系通訊大客戶強勁 AI 晶片測
試需求而積極採購高階測試設備。 4Q26 因實際投入量產的機台數量相對有
限，單季資本支出將較 3Q26 持平或微幅縮減，預估 2026 年資本支出約 130
億元。但 2027 年資本支出規模掌握度高且明確將超越 2026 年。主因龍潭新
廠單一樓層無塵室與機台設備支出即達 40 億元以上，公司規劃自 2Q27 起
每季開出 1 層全新無塵室加入量產， 2027 年約有 3 個樓層陸續投產，且後
續樓層的無塵室擴建將以現有主要客戶需求為優先配合對象。  
截至 3Q26 底，公司不動產、廠房及設備帳面價值擴張至 308.94 億元，佔總
資產比重達 64% ，資產總計同步推升至 485.56 億元。  
為支應擴產資金需求，公司除於 1Q26 至 3Q26 透過銀行借款淨增 61.43 億
元外，子公司於 09/2026 處分欣銓股票取得約 40 億元流動資金，後續將以
股利形式匯回母公司以支應資本支出。為因應 2027 年更為龐大的資本支出
銓現階段仍以銀行借款為優先籌資工具，預期負債比率將自 3Q26 底的 50%
進一步拉升，但公司承諾將整體負債比率嚴格控制在 60% 以內，並視擴產階
段適時評估其他籌資工具；同時因擴建資金留存考量，現金股利配發率預計
將由 2026 年的 70% 常態區間下修至 2027 年的 50% 以上。  
欣銓在海外生產基地的布局策略上，維持專注鞏固台灣、新加坡及中國南京
等現有廠區的營運效率與產能發揮。管理層明確指出，赴新國家或地區設立
生產基地必須具備足夠的在地客戶基礎與充沛的訂單量支撐，才能避免新
廠陷入長期營運虧損；基於當前尚未取得足額訂單承諾的考量，欣銓目前並
無赴美國投資建廠的計畫。  
在現有海外據點的產品與客戶定位方面，高階 AI ASIC 與先進製程相關的
測試訂單全數集中於台灣本地廠區，包括龍潭廠及全智科湖口廠。新加坡廠
則以承接成熟產品線為主，產品結構涵蓋車用、工控及消費性晶片，主要原
因在於新加坡當地目前尚無大量先進晶圓代工產能；後續欣銓在新加坡廠
區的成長策略，將聚焦於進一步深化與聯電 (UMC) 及世界先進 (VIS) 在新加
坡當地晶圓廠的委外測試合作機會。  
折舊費用方面，欣銓已公告自 1/1/2025 起，部分機器設備耐用年限由 7 年
延長至 9 年，這將有助於反映資產實際經濟效益，欣銓 2025 年折舊費用
31.39 億元。龍潭廠量產機台之折舊費用已有部分於 3Q26 開始提列，但因
部分設備驗收時點落於季中之後，折舊並未全額反映在 3Q26 單季，預期
4Q26 折舊費用將進一步高於 3Q26 ，預估 2026 年折舊費用約 35 億元。進入
2027 年後，新設備折舊將隨各季機台進場與驗收進度按部就班提列；依產
業慣例，新機台通常需於投產前一季進場進行裝機與驗證，例如 2Q27 投產
之產能將於 1Q27 進場裝機，驗收完成後即開始提列折舊。儘管折舊費用持
續墊高且 4Q26 至 1Q27 提前招募人力與產能整備將使毛利率自 3Q26 的
45.43% 高點略為回落，但因龍潭廠 4Q26 產能利用率維持滿載、全智湖口新
廠產能將於 2027 年達全產全銷，產能高稼動率預期可有效稀釋固定折舊成
本，使單季毛利率持續穩健維持在 40% 以上。  
產能利用率已達滿載，預估欣銓 4Q26 營收 55.24 億元， QoQ+3.50% ，稅前
淨利 18.57 億元， QoQ+1.75% ，稅後 EPS 3.03 元：  
展望 4Q26 營運，欣銓預估合併營收將呈現 QoQ+3% 左右，主要受到 3Q26

5 群益投顧 
營收基期墊高、歐美客戶於 11/2026 至 12/2026 進入假期拉貨淡季，以及手
機與通訊終端產品季節性調節所致。  
旗下全智總坪數逾 3,500 坪的湖口新廠，也於 3Q26 正式進入矽光子相關新
產品的量產階段，預期 4Q26 可以開出 20-25% 的產能，全智湖口廠客戶需
求高，因此 2027 年可以達到滿載狀況。  
毛利率展望方面，由於 4Q26 未有新無塵室開出，且公司需提前為 2027 年
擴產招募人力、提列初期整備成本與設備折舊增加，預期 4Q26 毛利率將略
低於 3Q26 之 45.43% ，但仍可穩健維持在高檔水準。定價策略方面，欣銓對
主力客戶價格維持穩定，但已針對低毛利與零星客戶提出調價，部分客戶已
順利反映漲價。公司努力進行成本管控和改善產品組合，讓 4Q26 獲利有機
會高於 3Q26。預估欣銓 4Q26 營收 55.24 億元， QoQ+3.50% ，稅前淨利 18.57
億元， QoQ+1.75% ，稅後 EPS 3.03 元。  
預估欣銓 2026 年營收 193.78 億元， YoY+38.13% ，稅前淨利 63.05 億元，
YoY+74.58% ，稅後 EPS 10.28 元。  
欣銓提供完整的光電測試 (全智 )與先進異質整合封裝 (瑞峰 )解決方案，建構
完整 CPO 生態系：  
欣銓集團在矽光子領域的布局完整，透過結合母公司與旗下轉投資公司的
資源，建構完整 CPO( 共封裝光學 )生態系，欣銓與全智皆為  SEMI 矽光子
產業聯盟及異質整合矽光子產業聯盟的重要成員。欣銓已布局光通訊與矽
光子 (CPO) 高階測試架構，目前接洽並配合之客戶達三家以上，測試製程至
少已切入至 Insertion 1 和 3 階段，並持續往 Insertion 4 進行技術評估與了
解。由於目前全球矽光子晶片出貨量仍處於技術開發與初期工程驗證階段，
市場尚未進入大規模營收爆發期，因此公司尚未設定具體之 CPO 營收貢獻
目標，但已透過全智湖口廠與美系通訊核心客戶建立緊密夥伴關係，提早卡
位高速傳輸光電整合封裝之長期測試商機。  
欣銓集團的矽光子佈局中，全智科技 (欣銓 100% 轉投資 )主要負責  EIC 測
試、PIC 測試以及光電整合測試 。 其中 EIC 測試已進入量產多年 ， 並於 2024 
年建置矽光子專用的光電整合測試實驗室，提供全方位的工程測試與驗證
服務。瑞峰半導體 (欣銓投資約  25%) 專注於先進異質整合封裝 (如 CPO 3D 
TSV/CoW ， EIC+PIC) 。瑞峰負責將 EIC 晶圓疊加在 PIC 晶圓上 (Chip on 
Wafer) ，隨後再交由全智進行光電整合測試。 Fabrinet( 策略投資夥伴 )主要負
責後續的  CPO 相關製程與光學引擎等項目。  
全智 (湖口新廠 )3Q26 正式量產，總面積超過  3,500 坪的湖口新廠，將著重
於矽光子測試。根據客戶端訊息，該廠區矽光子新產品的產能利用率拉升速
度預期將會相當快，且已與客戶洽談，有機會在  2027 年接近滿載。在  CPO 
的  EIC/PIC 測試產能上，目前已佈建超過  150 台設備。中長期擴廠計畫，
為因應強勁的光通訊客戶需求，湖口廠目前正在整理無塵室空間，希望能再
騰出 1~2 層樓來配合擴充；同時，為 2028 年以後的長期成長，公司也已準
備著手尋求購買新土地。  
瑞峰已於  2024 年建置第一條  12 吋矽光子晶圓級封裝 (WLP) 生產線。目
前將  EIC 疊在  PIC 晶圓上的  Chip on Wafer 製程正逐漸進入量產。 2025 
年已導入數家新客戶，進行超過  30 項  NPI( 新產品導入 )驗證； 2026 年更

6 群益投顧 
預計會有超過  10 家新客戶 (Tape-out / New design) 進行導入與設計中。  
預估欣銓 2027 年營收 251.13 億元， YoY+29.60% ，稅前淨利 81.39 億元，
YoY+29.08% ，稅後 EPS 13.20 元：  
展望 2027 年營運前景，欣銓管理層對整體營收增長維持正面樂觀基調，主
要受惠於 2 大核心擴產引擎全面啟動：第一為龍潭新廠無塵室自 2Q27 起進
入逐季開出階段，第二為子公司全智科技新竹湖口新廠產能將有機會達到
全產全銷。  
除晶圓代工廠外包訂單外，美系通訊核心客戶對高階 AI 晶片測試需求自
4Q26 一路強勁延伸至 2027 全年，全智湖口新廠產能已獲客戶全數包下，若
產能利用率達到滿載運作下，預期有機會貢獻 2027 年單月營收 1~1.5 億元。
車用與安控 (ATV/Security) 及儲存裝置 (Storage) 受惠於 Edge AI 與資料中心
需求回溫， 2027 年營收貢獻將持續擴張。  
季度走勢上，延續 4Q26 營收 QoQ+1~3% 的走勢， 1Q27 受到農曆春節工作
天數減少與季節性影響，預估營收將持平於 4Q26 ，毛利率受前期準備費用
影響維持在 40% 以上高檔水準，整體產能利用率 (UTR) 則維持約 80% 健康水
位。真正的成長將自 2Q27 啟動，欣銓龍潭廠共有 7 層無塵室，第 1 層已於
2Q26 年完成認證，自 07/2026 正式開始貢獻營收與量產。第 2 至第 3 層無
塵室工程預計於 2026 年底至 1H27 完工，其中第 2 層產能最快自 2Q27 開始
貢獻營運，依每季啟用一層的節奏逐步推進。  
獲利能力與定價方面，雖然新廠開出初期伴隨折舊與人力整備等成本，但隨
著 AI 測試需求暢旺帶動整體產能利用率持續站穩高檔，加上高階晶圓測試
比重維持高檔，評估 2027 年毛利率將高於 2026 年。此外，公司針對主要長
期配合之大型客戶維持穩定報價，但對於低毛利與零星客戶已啟動調價提
議並陸續反映漲價，進一步強化整體產品組合獲利體質。  
預估欣銓 2027 年營收 251.13 億元， YoY+29.60% ，稅前淨利 81.39 億元，
YoY+29.08% ，稅後 EPS 13.20 元。

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

## Document 185: 35 岁不会写代码？这波 AI 红利，可能就轮到你了

Source PDF: all report pdf/35 岁不会写代码？这波 AI 红利，可能就轮到你了.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

客⼾现在缺的，是既懂业务怎么运转、⼜能亲⼿把  AI 从⼀个想法做成上线系统的⼈。
摘要：
数据显⽰，全球科技公司年内已宣布裁员近  20 万⼈。与此同时，有⼀个  AI 岗位的招聘量却在
⼀年⾥暴涨了  7 倍多，薪资中位数折合⼈⺠币约  130 万元。

OpenAI 、 Anthropic 、微软、亚⻢逊、⾕歌，⼏家巨头前后砸下近百亿美元，有的开新公司，有
的建新部⻔，抢的都是这类⼈才。最反常识的是：这个岗位最稀缺的本事，恰恰不是写代码。

Anthropic ：培养  1 万个 “ 把  AI 送进企业的⼈ ”

10 ⽉  2 ⽇， Claude 的开发商  Anthropic 宣布投⼊  1 亿美元成⽴ “Claude 前沿学院 ” （ Claude
Frontier Academy ），⽬标是在  2027 年底前培养  1 万名 “ 前沿部署⼯程师 ” 。

⾸批学员是从合作伙伴中选出的在职⼯程师，来⾃埃森哲、摩根⼠丹利、诺和诺德和⼏家顶级
咨询公司。培训分两段：先是  4 天线下集训，从⼀次 “ 模拟企业部署 ” 开始；考核通过后，学员回
到⾃⼰公司，带⼀个真实项⽬做  12 周实战。⾸批认证预计在  2027 年初完成。

在  Anthropic 看来，企业落地  AI 的瓶颈不在模型，⽽在于缺少能推动变⾰的⼈。负责这个项⽬
的⾼管  Steve Corfield 说，客⼾现在缺的，是既懂业务怎么运转、⼜能亲⼿把  AI 从⼀个想法做
成上线系统的⼈。

这只是今年  AI 巨头抢⼈⼤战中的最新⼀例。

砸下百亿美元，巨头们都在抢同⼀种⼈才

今年  5 ⽉， OpenAI 成⽴了专⻔帮企业落地  AI 的 “OpenAI 部署公司 ” ，由  TPG 领投，⾸轮融资
超过  40 亿美元。同⽉， Anthropic 与⿊⽯、 Hellman & Friedman 、⾼盛合资成⽴了⼀家  AI 服
务公司，各⽅初始投资约  15 亿美元。

6 ⽉底到  7 ⽉初，亚⻢逊  AWS 和微软先后出⼿： AWS 投⼊  10 亿美元，组建⾃⼰的部署⼯程
团队；微软投⼊  25 亿美元成⽴新部⻔，把约  6000 名⼯程专家派驻到客⼾企业。 9 ⽉，⾕歌云
和埃森哲成⽴联合业务部⻔，计划培训最多  1000 名埃森哲⼯程师，专⻔派驻客⼾现场。

过去这些公司⽐的是谁的模型更强、参数更⼤、跑分更⾼，如今却不约⽽同地把钱砸向同⼀个
⽅向：派⼈进客⼾公司，把  AI 真正⽤起来。

这类⼈才在业内有个统⼀的叫法：FDE （ Forward Deployed Engineer ），中⽂常译作“ 前线部
署⼯程师 ”。

招聘量三年猛涨 30 倍，年薪中位数超百万元

熟悉美股的读者，对这个词的源头不会陌⽣。 FDE 最早是  Palantir （帕兰泰尔）在⼗多年前系
统化推⾏的⼀种做法：⼯程师不坐在总部写通⽤产品，⽽是常驻客⼾现场，和客⼾⽤同⼀套数
据、扛同⼀个业务指标，把模糊的需求、分散的数据和落不了地的⽬标，⼀点点变成真正能⽤
的系统。

当年很多⼈觉得这种模式太重、太慢，不像⼀家软件公司该⼲的事。今天回头看，这恰恰是
Palantir 最难被复制的护城河： 2026 年第⼆季度， Palantir 营收  19.35 亿美元，同⽐增⻓
93% ，其中美国商业收⼊同⽐增⻓  149% 。公司⾸席执⾏官  Alex Karp 有⼀个在业内被反复引⽤
的判断：⼤模型当然重要，但⾄少在未来⼏年⾥，价值在于落地实施。

⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

据劳动⼒市场分析机构  Lightcast 统计， 2023 年全美  FDE 招聘⼴告不到  200 条， 2025 年约
1200 条， 2026 年仅前九个⽉就已超过  7300 条。这些⼴告标出的薪资中位数约  19.5 万美元，
⽐  2023 年⾼出  40% ，是全美所有招聘⼴告薪资中位数的三倍多。 Indeed 的数据也指向同⼀个
⽅向：美国  FDE 职位从  2025 年  4 ⽉的  643 个增⾄  2026 年  4 ⽉的  5330 个，⼀年涨了  7 倍
多。

值得注意的是，今年前九个⽉发布  FDE 招聘⼴告最多的雇主中，德勤、埃森哲等咨询公司排在
了⾕歌等科技巨头之前。可⻅  FDE 已不只是  AI 公司的内部技术岗位，任何想把  AI ⽤进业务的
企业都需要这样的⼈。

需要说明的是， FDE 不等于 “ 驻场外包 ” 。外包是甲⽅说什么就做什么；FDE 则要先判断⼀件事
该不该⽤  AI 、⽤哪个模型、花多少钱、怎么证明有效，再把它真正落地上线。它要解决的是企
业⾥的⼀个⽼难题：懂技术的不懂业务，懂业务的不懂技术。

AI 落地难在业务，稀缺的恰恰是懂业务的⼈

很多⼈看到这⾥会想：这是年轻程序员的机会，我不写代码，也过了转⾏的年纪。

但不妨看看  Anthropic 怎么描述⼀个好的  FDE ：熟悉  Agent 系统、擅⻓解决问题、善于沟通、
有⼯程经验。这四条⾥，有⼀半在技术之外。

过去两年，⼏乎每家企业都买过  AI 产品、做过  AI 试点，但真正跑进⽇常业务的远⽐宣传的
少。⼏组被业内⼴泛引⽤的数据或许能说明⼀些问题：标普全球的调查显⽰，企业  AI 项⽬的弃
⽤率⼀年内从  17% 升到了  42%； Gartner 预计，到  2027 年超过四成的  Agent 项⽬会被取消；
IDC 与联想  2025 年的⼀份报告则显⽰，88% 的  AI 概念验证项⽬最终没有投⼊⽣产。

问题往往不出在模型上 —— 演⽰时⼀切顺利，⼀进⼊真实业务，就会碰上脏数据、⽼系统、权
限审批、员⼯习惯，以及 “ 出了错谁负责 ” 。毕⻢威的⼀位⾸席技术架构师举过⼀个例⼦：⼀个应
⽤先花了⼏个⽉梳理需求和业务背景，真正⽤  AI 开发只⽤了⼤约⼀个⽉。

FDE 真正值钱的那⼀半能⼒不是编程，⽽是懂业务。⼀份合同哪⼏条要紧，⼀张报表哪个数字
会出事，⼀个审批流程卡在哪⼀步，哪件事交给机器做会让⽼板睡不着觉，这些  AI 替代不了，
反⽽是在⾏业⾥⼲了⼗⼏、⼆⼗年的⼈最清楚。

你缺少的不是经验，⽽是把经验交给  AI 去执⾏的那⼀步：知道今天的  AI 能做什么、不能做什
么、该怎么搭、要花多少钱、出了错怎么兜底。

我们这次的训练营，就是为这⼀步准备的。

两天⾛完  AI 落地最后⼀公⾥

华尔街⻅闻⾸期「 FDE 实战训练营」将于  11 ⽉  14 ⽇⾄  15 ⽇在上海开班。这是⼀个线下实战
营，限量  50 席，在两天时间⾥，带你从零做出⼀个真正能⽤、能上线的  AI Agent 。

先说说  Agent （智能体）是什么。很多⼈对  AI 的印象还停留在 “ 问⼀句答⼀句 ” 的聊天上，但
Agent 不⼀样：你交给它⼀件事，它会⾃⼰查资料、打开软件、读写⽂件、交出结果，做错了还
能⾃⼰返⼯。巨头们派  FDE 进企业，部署的正是这种技术。

两天训练营共  12 个模块，从模型怎么选、价格怎么算，⼀直讲到评测、成本优化和安全上线，
都是⼤⼚把  AI 做上线时真正在⽤的⽅法。主讲讲师  Right 曾任腾讯⾼级算法⼯程师，主导过微
信核⼼  AI Agent 项⽬的算法设计和规模化落地，在  Agent 系统设计、模型训练、 Harness 架构
⽅向有深厚积累。他还在  NeurIPS 、 ACL 等顶级会议发表过多篇  Agent ⽅向论⽂，相关⼯作被
Google DeepMind 引⽤。

课程适合谁
企业主和管理者：想弄清  AI 到底能替公司做哪些事、每件事花多少钱、⻛险在哪⾥

### Page 3

⾦融、法务、财务、运营、咨询等专业⼈⼠：⼿上有⼤量重复⼯作，想亲⼿做⼀个⼯具交给
AI
想在  AI 时代给⾃⼰多留⼀项硬技能的⼈

三⼗年前，先学会⽤  Excel 的那批⼈，在后来的⼆⼗年⾥少熬了⽆数个夜。
今天巨头们花百亿美元在抢懂业务、⼜会⽤  AI 的⼈，⽽你已经有了⼀半。剩下那⼀半，就在这
两天⾥补⻬。

## Document 201: 7-9月期ユーロ圏GDP 減速しつつも上振れ

Source PDF: all report pdf/7-9月期ユーロ圏GDP　減速しつつも上振れ.pdf
Report source: DIR
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

欧州

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス 〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9 番 1 号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー 
このレポートは投資勧誘を意図して提供するものではありません。このレポートの掲載情報は信頼できると考えられる情報源から作成しておりますが、その正確性、完全
性を保証するものではありません。また、記載された意見や予測等は作成時点のものであり今後予告なく変更されることがあります。㈱大和総研と大和証券㈱は、㈱大和

2026 年 11 月 2 日 全6 頁

7-9 月期ユーロ圏 GDP 減速しつつも上振れ 
実質 GDP 成長率は前期比＋0.4％と市場予想を上回る

ロンドンリサーチセンター シニアエコノミスト 橋本 政彦

[要約] 
 ユーロ圏の2026 年7-9 月期の実質GDP 成長率（速報値）は前期比＋0.4％と、4-6 月期
（同＋0.6％）から成長ペースが鈍化しつつも、市場予想（Bloomberg 調査：同＋0.2％）
を上回る底堅い結果であった。 
 ユーロ圏の成長率の半分は、多国籍大 企業の影響を受けたアイルランドの高成長によ
るものである点は割り引いて見る必要がある。 ただし、アイルランドを除いてもプラ
ス成長を維持しており、 エネルギー高を背景としたインフレ率の加速や、記録的猛暑
による悪影響という逆風にもかかわらず、ユーロ圏経済は底堅く推移したという評価
に変わりはない。 
 国別の内訳では、スペインが内需の堅調さを主因として前期比＋0.6％と高成長を維持
し、ユーロ圏全体をけん引する構図が続いた。また、ドイツは同＋0.2％と、前期から
成長率は鈍化したものの、市場予想（同＋0.1％）を上回った。他方、フランスは同▲
0.1％と 2 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長に転じる低調な結果であった。 
 ユーロ圏経済は 10-12 月期も緩やかな景気拡大が続く見通しである。エネルギー価格
の上昇によるインフレ率の加速が個人消費を下振れさせるリスクには注意が必要だが、
ドイツを中心とした拡張財政がユーロ圏経済を下支えすると見込まれる。

### Page 2

1

2 / 6

成長の半分はアイルランドの上振れ、ただしそれを割り引いても底堅い 
ユーロ圏の 2026 年 7-9 月期の実質 GDP 成長率（速報値）は前期比＋0.4％と、4-6 月期（同
＋0.6％）から成長ペースが鈍化しつつも、市場予想（Bloomberg 調査、以下同：同＋0.2％）
を上回る底堅い結果であった。 
ただし、7-9 月期の上振れには、毎期の振れが非常に大きいアイルランドの高成長が影響し
ている点は割り引いて見る必要がある。 アイルランドの成長率は前期比 ＋5.5％と、成長率が
公表された 12 ヵ国の中で突出して高く、これがユーロ圏全体の成長 のおよそ半分を占めてい
る。CSO（アイルランド中央統計局 ）は、多国籍企業の影響が大きいセクターが押し上げ要因
になったと報告しており、一部の多国籍大企業の企業内取引などが成長率を押し上げたとみら
れる。アイルランドを除くユーロ圏 20 ヵ国の GDP 成長率（大和総研試算）は同＋0.2％となり、
実態としての景気回復ペースはヘッドラインよりも緩やかだったとみるべきだろう。もっとも、
アイルランドを除いてもプラス成長を維持しており、エネルギー高を背景としたインフレ率の
加速や、記録的猛暑による悪影響という逆風にもかかわらず、ユーロ圏経済は底堅く推移した
という評価に変わりはない。 
国ごとの成長率を確認 すると、スペイン、オランダ、エストニアがいずれも前期比＋ 0.6％
となり、既述のアイルランドの次に成長率が高かった。スペインの成長率は前期（同＋ 0.7％）
から減速したが、ユーロ圏全体に対する寄与度はアイルランドに次いで大きく、スペインの高
成長がユーロ圏全体をけん引する構図は7-9 月期も続いた。また、ドイツは同＋0.2％と、こち
らも前期（同＋0.3％）から成長ペースが鈍化しつつも、市場予想（同＋0.1％）を上回り、ユ
ーロ圏全体の成長を下支えした。他方、不調だったのがフランスであり、増加を見込んでいた
市場予想（同＋0.1％）に反して、同▲0.1％と2 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長に転じている。 
図表 1 ユーロ圏の実質 GDP 成長率と国別寄与度（左）、アイルランドの有無別 GDP 成長率（右）

（注）左図の「その他」は、ユーロ圏全体から表示された5 ヵ国（ドイツ、フランス、イタリア、スペイン、ア
イルランド）を差し引いて計算。 
（出所）Eurostat より大和総研作成

フランスは輸出・投資が減少、スペインは引き続き内需主導の成長維持 
ユーロ圏全体の 7-9 月期の需要項目別の内訳は速報段階で明らかになっていないため、以下
では内訳が明らかになっているフランスとスペインの需要項目別の動きを見ていく。 
フランスの実質 GDP 成長率は前期比▲0.1％と、2 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長となった。内・
外需別では、内需の前期比寄与度は＋0.5％pt とプラスだった一方、外需寄与度が同▲0.6％pt
となり GDP を押し下げた。内需の内訳では、在庫変動が最大の押し上げ要因（前期比寄与度：
＋0.4％pt）となった。ただし、内需から在庫を除いた国内最終需要も 前期からわずかに増加
ペースが加速しており、GDP 全体としてはマイナス成長ながら、内需については一定の底堅さ
が見られたと評価できる。 
需要項目別の詳細を見ていくと、個人消費は前期比 ＋0.4％と前期から加速し、これが内需
を押し上げた。増加幅は2024 年7-9 月期以来の大きさであり、個人消費に限れば7-9 月期はむ
しろ好調だった と評価できる。内訳 では、財（同＋0.4％） 、サービス（同＋0.4％）がいずれ
も前期から加速しており、 個人消費は幅広く増加し ている。財消費では、エネルギーが同＋
1.2％と増加に転じたことが主な押し上げ 要因となった 。ガソリン価格 が上昇する中、石油精
製品の減少は続いたものの、電力消費の増加がエネルギー全体を押し上げた。また、エネルギ
ー以外の財では 、電気自動車の販売増加により、輸送用機器が同＋3.1 ％と好調だった。サー
ビス消費では、娯楽関連を中心とした家庭向けサービスの増加（同＋0.9％）、および宿泊・飲
食サービス（同＋0.7％）が主な押し上げ要因となった。 
好調だった個人消費とは対照的に、総固定資本形成 は前期比▲0.6％と 3 四半期連続で減少
し、内需の足を引っ張った 。最大の押し下げ要因となったのは、 輸送用機器が同▲7.0％と大
幅に減少したことである。統計公表元の INSEE（フランス国立統計経済研究所）の報告によれ
ば、前期にあったクルーズ船の受け渡しの反動、および企業による投資の減少が押し下げ 要因
となった。このほか、建設投資が同▲0.5％と3 四半期連続で減少したほか、不動産取引の停滞
により、法律サービスなどのビジネスサービス（同▲ 1.6％ ）、不動産サービス （同▲0.7％）
が前期から減少した。他方、IT 機器の増加により資本財が同＋0.8％と増加したほか、サービ
ス投資の中でも情報通信サービスは同＋2.1％と増加した。 
外需に関して、輸出は前期比▲1.7％と2 四半期ぶりに減少した。前期に大幅に増加した航空
関連が反動で 同▲9.1 ％と大きく減少したことが減少の主因と なった。 また、 農産品 （同▲
7.0％）、農作物加工品（同▲1.1％）が減少したほか、資本財が同▲1.1％と 7 四半期ぶりの減
少に転じるなど 、財輸出が総じて振るわなかった。 サービス輸出は同＋0.5％と増加を維持し
たが、増加幅は前期（同＋1.0％）から減速している。 
輸入は前期比＋0.1％と、小幅ながら増加した。自動車、および海軍装備品の増加によって
輸送用機器が同＋0.3％増加したことや、資本財の増加（同＋1.4％）が押し上げに寄与した。
他方、石油精製品（同▲6.4 ％ ）、農作物加工品（同▲1.6％）が減少したことで 、輸入全体の
増加幅は小幅にとどまった。

4 / 6

図表 2 フランス・スペインの実質 GDP 成長率と需要項目別寄与度

（出所）INSEE、INE、Haver Analytics より大和総研作成

スペインの実質 GDP 成長率は前期比＋0.6％となり、4-6 月期（同＋0.7％）から成長ペース
が減速しつつも、高い成長率を維持した。内 ・外需別では、内需 の前期比寄与度が ＋0.5％pt、
外需が同＋0.1％pt となり、引き続き堅調な内需が成長のドライバーとなっている。 
個人消費は前期比＋0.6％となった。形態別の内訳では、サービスが同＋0.9％と、15 四半期
連続で増加し好調さを維持したことに加え、耐久財が同＋0.5％と2 四半期連続で増加し、非耐
久財は同＋0.6％と 4 四半期ぶりの増加に転じている。他方、半耐久財は同▲2.2％と減少に転
じた。 
総固定資本形成は前期比＋0.7 ％と、11 四半期連続 の増加を維持した。ただし、増加幅は
2025 年 4-6 月期以来の小ささとなり、増加ペースは鈍化している。建設投資が同＋1.0％と堅
調さを維持する一方で、輸送用機械の減少が下押し要因となり、機械・設備・武器システムは
同＋0.2％と小幅な増加にとどまった。 
外需に関して、輸出は前期比＋1.3％と4 四半期連続で増加し、増加幅が前期から拡大した。
インバウンド（非居住者による支出）の増加（ 同＋1.6％）を中心に、サービス 輸出が同＋
1.5％と増加し、輸出全体をけん引する状況が続いている が、サービス輸出 の伸びは前期から
縮小した。他方、財輸出は同＋1.1％と、増加幅はサービス輸出に比べて小さいものの、前期
から増加ペースが加速している。 
輸入は前期比＋1.1％となった。サービス輸入が同＋2.1％と大きく増加したことに加え、財
輸入も同＋0.8％と増加した。輸入の増加幅は前期から縮小し、輸出の増加幅を下回ったため、
外需（純輸出）の寄与度は2 四半期ぶりのプラスに転じている。 
なお、ドイツ （前期比＋0.2 ％）については、まだ内訳の詳細な計数は公表されていないが、

ドイツ連邦統計局からは、消費支出の増加が押し上げに寄与したこと、一方で総固定資本形成
が減少したことが報告されている。また、輸出については好調だった2026 年上期から減速した
とコメントされた。イタリア（同横ばい）については、内需寄与度のプラスと、外需寄与度の
マイナスがイタリア国家統計局によって報告されている。これらの情報を総合すると、7-9 月
期は、個人消費を中心とした内需がユーロ圏の成長に寄与した可能性が高い。

防衛・インフラ投資が景気拡大を下支え、リスクはエネルギー価格 
ユーロ圏経済の 10-12 月期に入ってからの景気動向に関して、欧州委員会が公表するユーロ
圏景況感指数（総合）を確認すると、2026 年10 月は前月差▲0.6pt と2 ヵ月連続で低下した。
もっとも、過去の関係に照らせば10 月の指数の水準は、景気悪化を示すほどの低さではなく、
ユーロ圏経済は引き続き緩やかなペースでの拡大が続いていることを示唆する。 
景況感指数の内訳を見ると、消費者信頼感指数が前月差▲1.1pt と 2 ヵ月連続で低下したほ
か、これに平仄を合わせる形で小売業が同▲1.5pt と低下した。これは、エネルギー価格の上
昇によってインフレ率のさらなる加速に対する懸念が高まっていることや、生活不安を理由 と
した各地での抗議活動の広がりが影響したとみられる。実際、10 月に入って大規模なデモや暴
動が発生したフランスの消費者信頼感指数は、同▲3.4pt と主要国の中でも目立って大きく前
月から悪化した。サービス業の景況感は依然として高水準ながら、同▲0.6pt と 6 ヵ月ぶりの
低下に転じたのも、消費者マインドの悪化や、それを背景とした需要の減少が下押し要因とな
った可能性が高い。

図表 3 ユーロ圏の景況感指数と実質 GDP 成長率（左） 、景況感指数の内訳（右）

（出所）Eurostat、欧州委員会より大和総研作成

一方、家計部門の弱含みとは対照的に、鉱工業の景況感はこのところ堅調さを増している。
鉱工業の景況感指数は、前月差＋0.5pt と 5 ヵ月連続で改善し、2023 年 4 月以来の高さとなっ
た。鉱工業の景況感の回復には、拡張財政による防衛支出・インフラ投資関連の需要の増加が

寄与しているとみられる。足元では長期金利が上昇ペースを速めており、これが設備投資や公
共投資の拡大を抑制する恐れはあるものの、積極的に財政を拡張 させるドイツを中心とした防
衛支出、インフラ投資がユーロ圏経済を下支えする構図は当面続くと見込まれる。 
先行きのリスク要因としては、引き続きエネルギー価格の動向、およびその影響の広がりが
焦点となる。今回の 7-9 月期の GDP 統計では、インフレ率が加速する中でも個人消費が底堅く
推移したことが確認された。だが、原油やガスなどのエネルギー価格は上昇が続いており、実
質所得や消費者マインドへの下押し圧力は、今後さらに強まる可能性がある。とりわけ冬季の
需要期にはエネルギー価格上昇による負担感が一層強まるとみられ、個人消費の下振れに警戒
する必要があるだろう。

### Page 1

2026.10.30   
한화에어로스페이스  
(012450)

3Q26: 그래도 믿는다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원 
한화에어로스페이스의 3분기 매출은 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원(OPM 7.4%)로 
컨센서스와 당사 추정치를 모두 대폭 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다. 앞서 실적을 발표한 
한화오션의 실적 쇼크를 감안하더라도, 주력사업인 지상방산 부문의 실적이 매출 1조 
2,455억원/영업이익 217억원(1.7%)로 지난 분기(2조 1,075억원/ 5,330억원/ 25.3%) 
는 물론 전년동기(2조 1,098억원/5,725억원/27.1%)보다도 현저히 감소하였다. 
일회성 요인은 대전사업장 폭발사고에 따른 복구비용 등 300억원, 일부 국내사업 
충당금 160억원 및 환율하락에 따른 효과 등이 있었으나 역시 이를 감안해도 내수와 
수출 모두 실질 이익률은 5%미만으로 추정된다. 지상방산 분기 매출은 1분기(1조 
2,211억)보다 오히려 소폭 증가하였으나 영업이익이 1분기 2,087억원에서 
217억원으로 대폭 감소한 점에 대하여 회사는 프로젝트별 믹스 구성에 따른 낮은 
이익률의 인도분이 반영되었다고 설명하고 있으나, 그럼에도 불구하고 수출과 내수 
이익률 모두 BEP에 가까웠다는 점은 쉽게 이해가 가지는 않는다. 다만 방산기업의 
특성상 대전사업장 사고에 따른 실제 납기 영향 등 회사가 공개할 수 없는 사정이 
있었을 것으로 추측할 뿐이다. 대전사업장은 현재 복구작업이 진행 중이며 27년 
상반기 중에는 가동 정상화할 것으로 예상된다.  
긍정적인 소식도 있다. 폴란드향 천무의 연간 인도대수가 기존 가이던스인 40대에서 
60대로 증가하였으며, 이를 포함하여 3분기에 인도되지 않은 물량의 대부분이 
4분기에 집중될 예정으로, 올해의 4분기 실적 개선폭은 통상적으로 볼 수 있는 
4분기의 계절적 집중도보다 더 커질 것으로 예상한다. 방산기업의 실적은 인도일정에 
따라 크게 변할 수 있는 분기 실적보다 연간 합산이 더 중요하다는 점을 한 번 더 
믿고, 4분기를 기다리는 게 좋겠다.

투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 1,200,000원 하향  
투자의견 BUY를 유지하지만, SOTP기반 목표주가는 기존 1,490,000원에서 
1,200,000원으로 19.5% 하향한다. 비영업가치 중 자회사 한화시스템과 한화오션, 
쎄트렉아이의 지분가치 하락(합산 8.7조원→7.1조원), 그리고 지난 분기 대비 늘어난 
별도 순차입금(3.25조→4.95조원)을 반영하였으며 최근 방산섹터 전반의 밸류 하락을 
감안하여 영업가치 중 지상방산+항공우주의 Target P/E를 기존 35배에서 30배로 
조정하였다. 
3분기는 여러모로 악재가 겹쳤다. 주식시장 전반의 하락에 더해 방산섹터 수주 지연에 
따른 디레이팅, 그리고 납기 지연에 따른 실적 하락 등 나올 수 있는 악재는 다 
나왔다고도 볼 수 있다. 그러나 뒤집어보면 지연중인 수주는 시간의 문제일 뿐 가시화 
가능성이 매우 높으며, 실적 또한 진행률 인식이 아닌 인도기준 인식에 따른 일시적 
현상으로 4분기 이후 복구될 가능성이 크다. 펀더멘털의 유의미한 변화는 없다고 볼 
수 있으며 밸류에이션 매력도가 증가한 현 시점에서 추가 매수를 고려할 때다. 다만, 
대전사업장 사고에 따른 당국의 제재조치가 아직 확정되지 않았음은 잔여 리스크 
요인이다. 동종사의 신규계약 입찰제한 제재조치가 주가에 상당한 영향을 미쳤음을 
고려하면 한화에어로스페이스 또한 제재조치 여부를 지켜본 후 투자할 것을 권한다.

당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
 당 연구원은 한화에어로스페이스(012450)의 일부 비용을 지원받아 한화에어로스페이스 공장 투어(2026년 10월 1-2)를 다녀왔습니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상 
산업추천 투자등급  
시가총액기준 산업별 시장비중대비 보유비중의 변화를 추천하는 것임 
 Overweight(비중확대) 
 Neutral(중립) 
 Underweight(비중축소)

### Page 1

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발적 
2026-10-29

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 640,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 285,500 원 
상승여력: 124.2%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 감소 및 환율 하락으로 시장 예상치 하회 
Key: 4Q26~1Q27부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 수주 모멘텀 전망 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 엔진&FDC으로 밸류 매력도 충분. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 시장예상치 하회하는 실적 시현  
3Q26 연결 매출액 5조 7,536억원(+30.2% YoY, -9.1% QoQ), 영업이익 8,992억원
(+61.3% YoY, -13.5% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(9,792억원)를 하회했다. 조업일수 감소
(휴가 10일+중대재해 4일) 및 환율 하락 영향(450억원)과 더불어, 임단협 관련 예상 비
용 중 75%를 이번 분기 선반영 했기 때문이다. 상선 수주 빈티지는 중형 선박 기준 ‘23년 
9.6%, ‘24년 65%, ‘25년 25%, 대형 선박 기준 ‘22년 4%, ‘23년 45%, ‘24년 31%, 
‘25년 20%로, 선가 상승효과 지속되는 중이다. 특히 해양 부문에서 루야 프로젝트의 공정
률 확대 효과 및 C/O로 180억원의 환입 발생으로 전 분기 대비 큰 폭의 이익 성장 시현했
다. 최근 키윗과 미국 내 합작 조선소 설립을 추진하며, 미 군수지원함 초도 2척 수주 및 향
후 물량 건조, 그리고 HII와의 함정 선체블록 하도급 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 64 만원으로 하향 
HD현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 82만원에서 64만원
으로 하향한다. 최근 상선 실적 피크아웃 우려에 따른 조선주 주가 급락으로 멀티플 하향 조
정했다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 
FDC에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지속되고 있는 중이다. 올해 공시 기
준으로 육상발전 엔진 약 1.6GW 수주한 것으로 알려졌으나, 미공시된 약 1.4GW까지 포
함 시 총 3.0GW 이상을 수주한 상황이다. 캐파 증설 기준으로 ‘28 년 1.5GW, ‘29 년 
3GW 캐파에 해당하는 물량을 이미 대부분 채웠으며, 기존 계획 대비 빠른 준공 혹은 공정
률 확대, 더 나아가 추가적인 캐파 증설까지 고려할 수준으로 파악된다. 빠르면 올해 연말 
혹은 내년 초부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 본격 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 
전망이다. 현 주가 레벨에서 상승여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 3Q 실적 및 임단협 불
확성 해소된 현 시점에서 적극적 매수 의견 및 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)로 제시한다.

본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.

## Document 355: Google cloud beats but rest sucks

Source PDF: all report pdf/Google cloud beats but rest sucks.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    陸⾏之  Andrew Lu on global semis and techs <andrewhclu@substack.com>
Subject: Google cloud beats but rest sucks
Date:    19 minutes ago
Google cloud beats but rest sucks
View in browser
Google cloud beats but rest
sucks
⾕歌雲的營收增⻑及資本開⽀回報率都在加速，但營收佔⽐太低無法
⼤幅改善低於預期的舊業務發展，也無法沖銷⾮核⼼業務的⼤幅虧損
ANDREW LU ON GLOBAL SEMI/TECHS
OCT 28 ∙ PAID
READ IN APP
Executive summary
雖然⾕歌雲及⾃研晶⽚ TPUs 的營收，獲利及資本⽀出營收及獲利回報率都在
加速，但因為營收佔⽐太低，加上龐⼤的舊業務不僅營收增速緩慢外⽽且還低
於市場預期，這讓整體營收無法提供投資⼈任何驚喜，外加⼀些⾮核⼼業務虧

損擴⼤將⾕歌雲的獲利貢獻全部消耗殆盡，盤後回檔符合預期。以後要持續關
注佔⽐僅整體營收 1/4 的⾕歌雲發展及⾮核⼼業務虧損是否持續擴⼤，尤其是
關注⾃研晶⽚ TPU 銷售及外租對獲利的影響。
The rest sucks
雖然⾕歌  3Q26 的服務營收仍有 10.6% 的年增，但⽐起  Google Cloud 倍增
的營收明顯不⾜，⽽其中不管是搜尋引擎， Youtube 油管業務都低於預期近
2% ，服務業務營業利潤率也低於預期 1 個百分點，這讓服務業務營業利潤⽐市
場預期低了近 3% 。
除此以外，佔⽐不⼤的其他業務  Other Bets ，營業虧損持續擴⼤，從去年的
同期的 14 億美元營業虧損，擴⼤⾄  3Q26 的 20 億美元的營業虧損（ 19% 虧損
⾼於彭博社分析師預期）；加上⾕歌將發⽣在⺟公司的  AI 研發費⽤獨⽴列表
（其中包括員⼯激勵費⽤，，營業虧損從去年同期的 45 億美元，擴⼤⾄  3Q26
的 77 億美元（ 40% 虧損⾼於分析師預期），最後加上⼀筆近 82 億美元的⾮經常

性業外費⽤，這三項費⽤的加總將  Google Cloud 的獲利貢獻完全抵銷殆盡，
投資⼈以後要關注這些虧損是否持續擴⼤。

Growth all depends on Cloud
Google Cloud sales growth is accelerating: 在算⼒基礎建設
持續擴充及 bn tokens per minute (32bn tokens per minute in
3Q26 from 22bn tokens in 2Q26 ，季增 45%) ⾕歌雲  3Q26 業務不但
年度倍增⾄ 307 億美元（季增 24% ）也⾼於彭博社分析師預期近 6% ，即
使營收年增持續加速（ 82% yoy in 2Q26, 103% yoy in 3Q26) ，佔⽐
拉⾼從去年同期的 15% ，拉⾼⾄  3Q26 的 24% ，但還是無法明顯拉升整

體營收（年增 25% ，季增 6% ）。在營業利潤率⽅⾯，⾕歌雲於  3Q26 近
36% 的營業利潤率已經是 2 個季度⾼於公司整體營業利潤率的 32-34% ，
也明顯⾼於彭博社分析師預期的 32.5% 。⾕歌雲的  Rule of 40% （營收
年增率 + 營業利潤率）在 3Q26 持續增加⾄ 139% ，⾼於 2Q26 的
117% ，及去年同期的 57% 。
No more capex raised: 不同於前幾個季度持續上修資本開⽀，⾕歌
維持  2026 全年 1950-2050 億美元資本開⽀，但 3Q26 季度 514 億美元
的資本開⽀實際上是低於市場預期達 8% ，這讓資本開⽀佔⾕歌雲營收⽐

### Page 3

從 2Q26 的 181% ，降低到  3Q26 的 167% ，即使資本開⽀佔整體營收⽐
仍從 2Q26 的 38% 逐季攀升到  40% 。
Capital return on cloud sales and proﬁts improved
consecutively: ⾕歌雲的資本開⽀營收回報率從上季度的 55% ，增加
到這季度 3Q26 的 67% ，整體營收的資本開⽀的營收回報率從上季度的
46% ，暴增到這季度的 111% 。⾕歌雲的資本開⽀獲利回報率從上季度的
27% ，逐步增加到這季度 3Q26 的 29% ，這表⽰公司的資本開⽀能在 4 年
內透過獲利回收。
Expanding TPUs business- two news in one day
⾕歌宣布  3Q26 ⾕歌雲的營收倍增，除了 AI 基礎建設及解決⽅案外，也包括
TPU 的系統銷售。根據  PR Newswire 正式公告、Oracle 官⽅新聞發布 與
Google Cloud 新聞中⼼，甲⾻⽂（ Oracle, NYSE: ORCL ）與⾕歌

（ Google, NASDAQ: GOOGL ）於  2026 年  10 ⽉  27 ⽇在拉斯維加斯舉⾏
的「 Oracle AI World 」⼤會上宣布  Google ⾃研張量處理器（ TPU ）將正式
進駐  Oracle Cloud Infrastructure （ OCI ）。這是  Google 歷史上⾸次將其核
⼼專屬  TPU 算⼒授權並部署於  Google 體系之外的超⼤規模雲端平台。雙⽅
規劃⾃  2027 年起在  OCI 全⾯商轉第⼋代  TPU 叢集，包含針對前沿模型訓練
的  TPU 8t Zebraﬁsh （算⼒密度達前代近  3 倍，由聯發科提供設計統包⼯
程）以及針對⾃主多代理⼯作流的  TPU 8i Sunﬁsh 推論晶⽚（由博通提供設
計統包⼯程），由  OCI 提供⼤規模機房⽔冷、電⼒與超⾼頻寬  RDMA 網絡，
共同打破專屬硬體孤島。
根據  BusinessWorld 引述路透社與  The Information 最新調查，全球  AI 領

頭⽺  OpenAI 正積極推進直接採購  Google ⾃研張量處理器（ TPU ）的專案
計畫，標誌著  OpenAI 與  Alphabet/Google 兩⼤死敵在激烈競爭之餘，正式
建⽴起深度的硬體基礎設施採購聯盟。這項採購案緊隨上週  OpenAI 宣布將模
型上架  Google Cloud Model Garden 、以及昨⽇甲⾻⽂  OCI 宣布引進
Google TPU 之後爆發。⾯對微軟、亞⾺遜與  Alphabet 2026 年合計⾼達
6,000 億美元的瘋狂資本⽀出， OpenAI 急於尋求性價⽐更⾼、功耗更低的替
代算⼒以擺脫對輝達  GPU 的單⼀重度依賴。 Alphabet 財務⻑  Anat
Ashkenazi 證實， TPU 外部銷售預計⾃  2026 年底開始認列⼩部分營收，並
將在  2027 年全⾯爆發。

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
The six-month annualised growth rate in the Westpac–
Melbourne Institute Leading Index, which indicates the
likely pace of economic activity relative to trend three to
nine months into the future, held at –0.03% in September,
unchanged from August.

The Leading Index continues to show moderately improved
momentum compared to the middle of the year, with the
latest reads pointing to growth holding at or slightly below
trend heading into late 2026/early 2027. Growth appears
to be stabilising at a slow pace rather than stalling. That
said, both the latest fuel prices and the RBA’s September
interest rate increase are still working their way through to
the economy with impacts likely to take some of the gloss off

growth near term. Overall, Westpac expects annual growth
to come in at 1.5% for calendar 2026, a below-trend result.
The Leading Index growth rate has firmed slightly over the
last six months, rising from –0.16% back in March to –0.03%
in September. The component detail shows mixed moves over
the period. Four components have contributed more positively
with reduced drags from the Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Unemployment Expectations Index (adding +0.23ppts to

the Index growth rate); the Westpac-Melbourne Institute
Consumer Expectations Index (adding another +0.15ppts);
and the S&P/ASX200 (+0.15ppts) combining with a modest
lift in growth in US industrial production (adding +0.12ppts).
These positives have been partially offset by modest drags
from four previously stable components: dwelling approvals
(–0.34ppts); monthly hours worked (–0.08ppts); commodity
prices, measured in AUD terms (–0.07ppts) and the yield

spread (–0.05ppts).
Overall, the component mix speaks to a lack of direction.
Indeed, despite some large shocks over the last year, the
Leading Index growth rate has been relatively contained since
early 2025, holding in a narrow –0.3% to +0.5% range. This
compares to history which, outside of the COVID and GFC
shocks, has seen growth rates move between a much wider
–1.9% to +1.6% range. Of course, the apparent stability,
which is evident across all components of the index, may not

last but for now it suggests the economy has been stuck in a
bit of a holding pattern over the last two years.
Leading Index still tracking a touch below trend
The Westpac-Melbourne Institute Leading Index of
Economic Activity is designed to assist in identifying
turning points in the economy. The index combines
variables that reflect different aspects of the economy
into a single index that generally produces a more reliable
cyclical indicator than any single component taken

individually. The Leading Index of Economic Activity
provides advance information on the state of the economy
and gives early warnings of cyclical turning points.
Matthew Hassan
Head of Australian Macro-Forecasting
**Please note that this will be the last Westpac press release for
the monthly Westpac-Melbourne Institute Leading Index. We will
continue to publish regular bulletin updates through our westpaciq.
com.au website.

## Document 411: Taiwan Earnings Call Weekly Recap - Is Earnings Still Enough to Drive Stock Price_ _ GSTW

Source PDF: all report pdf/Taiwan Earnings Call Weekly Recap - Is Earnings Still Enough to Drive Stock Price_ _ GSTW.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Earnings Call Weekly Recap - Is Earnings
Still Enough to Drive Stock Price? | GSTW
30 October 2026 | 4:17 PM Taiwan Standard Time
Robert Wang
Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C. Taipei Branch
FICC & Equities
Short week but busy for Taiwan in terms of earnings. Index is only down 1.8% WTD,
though multiple large caps saw over 10% drawdown in this week alone. The elevated
level in Taiwan vs other regional markets still attracted much short interest without

much buying interest except from retail. Overall sentiment is not bearish, though
investors have turned much more defensive and hiding in sectors such as Financials,
Telecom, and TSMC.
For earnings, winners' momentum was short lived; instead of chasing into the beat,
many chose to further profit take on the strength (Unimicron, EMC/TUC, Landmark
on open right after their earnings). Companies that missed the results were definitely
punished (i.e. Chroma, Delta, UMC). Echoing back to my previous note, range bound

trade seems to be the safest way to stay afloat.
See below for my single stock takeaway:
Mediatek (2454 TT)
Financial Highlights:
3Q26 revenue reached NT$168.06bn (+10% QoQ and +18% YoY) with EPS at
NT$17.5 (+15% QoQ and +11% YoY) vs cons. NT$15.95. GM came in at 46.3%
(+11ppt QoQ and 18ppt YoY) vs cons. 46.2%.
3Q26 outperformance was mainly from stronger-than-expected customer
demand and improved supply chain yield. First ASIC already started shipping since

Sep'26.
Operational Outlook:
Revenue outlook for 4Q26 is NT$210 - NT$222bn, translating to a 29% sequential
growth. 2027 revenue growth will likely grow by high single digits yoy. GM will be
around 45% with +/-1.5%, and OPM is expected to be at 26% +/-2%.
ASIC chip sales for 2026 will be around US$2.5bn, higher than previous guidance,
and it will reach US$16bn in 2027, around 20% of ASIC TAM and at the higher end
of the company guidance. Next gen chip's tape out is on track and targeted to

enter mass production in 1Q28.
Delta Electronics (2308 TT)
10/30/26, 4:46 PM Taiwan Earnings Call Weekly Recap - Is Earnings Still Enough to Drive Stock Price? | GSTW
https://marquee.gs.com/s/content/markets/2971c821-c2cc-403a-acfe-2b906dd125c4 1/11

### Page 2

Financial Highlights:
3Q26 revenue reached NT$197.1bn (+8% QoQ / +31% YoY), while EPS registered
at NT$9.05. GM declined to 34.1% (down 1.5ppt QoQ), impacted by a NT$3.9bn
inventory write-down (~1.7% of revenue) mostly in Power Electronics.
Operating profit rose +7% QoQ (+31% YoY) backed by operating leverage, keeping
OPM resilient at 16.6% flat QoQ. Net profit was NT$23.5bn (-7% QoQ / +26% YoY)
due to lower non-operating investment gains.
Operational Outlook:

Management expects 4Q26 revenue to grow sequentially with gross margins
unlikely to deteriorate, aided by inventory normalization and potential price
adjustments to offset ~2% YTD material cost inflation.
AI-related exposure approaches 50% of total revenue (liquid cooling at ~13%), and
management remains confident in AI infrastructure demand through 2030 despite
near-term bottlenecks.
Key Q&A:
Inventory Write-down & Normalization: The NT$3.9bn write-down stemmed from

component mismatches and slower customer ramp-ups of newer platforms (e.g.,
18.5kW vs. 5.5kW platforms); management expects affected inventory to be
progressively consumed by 1Q27.
HVDC Adoption & Vicor Litigation: While 400V products saw partial shipments in
3Q26, 800V adoption is slower and primarily NVIDIA-driven. On the Vicor VPD
patent dispute, management expects no near-term impact on customer
relationships or shipments, with an initial ITC determination unlikely until 2027.

ASE Technology Holding (3711 TT)
Financial Highlights:
3Q26 consolidated revenue reached NT$244.16bn, with a GM of 22.1% and EPS of
NT$5.84, all outperforming market expectations.
ATM (Advanced Semiconductor Engineering, Test and Materials) GM hit 30.6%,
breaking past previous structural highs; the company announced 30% as its
baseline gross margin for the future.
Operational Outlook:
4Q26 consolidated revenue is projected to grow 6%–8% QoQ, with GM between

23.0%–24.0% and OPM between 14.5%–15.5%.
CPO is set to enter mass production in 4Q26, with related revenue expected to
contribute over US$500mn in 2027; the full-year LEAP revenue target has been
revised upward to over US$4bn.
Key Q&A:
Capex Upward Revision: Due to strong demand for LEAP and mainstream
advanced packaging, full-year 2026 capex was raised from US$10.5bn to
US$11.5bn (60% allocated to LEAP, 40% to mainstream packaging), with 2027
capex expected to be even higher.

Capacity Bottlenecks & Expansion: Factory floor space is currently the primary
constraint on near-term growth. The company is actively searching for and
acquiring existing facilities to shorten setup timelines, prioritizing resource
allocation toward packaging and wafer sort.
Elite Material (2383 TT)
10/30/26, 4:46 PM Taiwan Earnings Call Weekly Recap - Is Earnings Still Enough to Drive Stock Price? | GSTW
https://marquee.gs.com/s/content/markets/2971c821-c2cc-403a-acfe-2b906dd125c4 2/11

### Page 3

Financial Highlights:
Driven by strong demand, actual 3Q26 revenue grew 29% QoQ, comfortably
beating prior conservative expectations.
GM outperformed expectations, hitting 37.7% for the quarter.
Operational Outlook:
4Q26 revenue growth is conservatively estimated to be at least 15%–20% QoQ,
with gross margins expanding further to 38.0%–39.5%.
Proactive capacity expansion plans (targeting 15.25 million sheets/month by end-
2030 vs. 5.85 million sheets/month currently) are still insufficient to support all

signed Long-Term Agreements (LTAs) for the next 4 years, pointing to a severe
supply-demand gap and zero oversupply risk.
Key Q&A:
CCL Pricing & Substrate Expansion: EMC is executing its second round of high-end
CCL (M7+) price hikes this year (5%–20% increases covering M7/M8 and M8+/M9
grades). Furthermore, it is rapidly expanding high-end ABF substrate core-layer
CCL capacity from 300k sheets/month to at least 1.2 million sheets/month by end-
2027, targeting commercial shipments starting in 4Q27.

Competition & Market Share: While AI customers are qualifying second or third
sources to secure supply chains, EMC maintains its long-term target of holding a
50% AI CCL market share, supported by best-in-class product quality and deep
CSP/PCB collaboration.
Yageo Corporation (2327 TT)
Financial Highlights:
3Q26 revenue reached NT$49.53bn (+11.4% QoQ, +49.7% YoY), with a GM of 41%
and EPS of NT$5.83.
Free cash flow surged to NT$9.236 billion, while the net financial debt-to-EBITDA

ratio dropped from 59% in 4Q25 to 24% in 3Q26, reflecting a continuously
improving financial structure and robust cash generation.
Operational Outlook:
4Q26 revenue, GM, and OPM are all expected to edge up sequentially, defying
typical seasonal flat-to-down trends for the fourth quarter.
AI demand remains solid, with its contribution to total revenue expanding from
15% in 1Q26 and 16% in 2Q26 to approximately 20% in 3Q26.
Key Q&A:
Capacity & Expansion Plans: Capacity utilization rates stood at 95% in 3Q26 and

are expected to exceed 95% in 4Q26, surpassing the healthy range of 85%–90%.
Total capacity for tantalum capacitors and MLCCs is planned to expand by 10%–
15% in 2027, with portions coming online starting in the second quarter.
Order Momentum & B/B Ratio: The overall book-to-bill (B/B) ratio in 3Q26
remained close to 2.0, with AI-related tantalum capacitors and MLCCs seeing
particularly robust orders, while LTAs with core customers continue to underpin

future shipments.
Nan Ya Plastics (1303 TT)
Financial Highlights:
10/30/26, 4:46 PM Taiwan Earnings Call Weekly Recap - Is Earnings Still Enough to Drive Stock Price? | GSTW
https://marquee.gs.com/s/content/markets/2971c821-c2cc-403a-acfe-2b906dd125c4 3/11

### Page 4

3Q26 revenue rose to NT$95.6bn (+14.3% QoQ), with electronic materials
expanding to 58.1% of total sales. Operating profit surged 61.2% QoQ to
NT$18.31bn, of which electronic materials contributed 93.8%.
EPS hit a record NT$4.90, bringing 9M26 EPS to a record NT$10.07, supported by
electronic material profitability and investment income from Nanya Technology.
Operational Outlook:
CCL has surpassed NYPCB as the segment's primary revenue driver (31% of

electronic materials) with segment OPM scaling to 30.9% (up from 19.9% in 2Q26).
Management guided for fiberglass fabrics and CCL price hikes to continue at a 5–
10% MoM increase pace through early 2027, alongside a notable product mix shift
toward M7/M8 in 1Q27.
Key Q&A:
CCL Pricing and M-Series Mix: CCL and fiberglass-cloth pricing remains firm into
early 2027 with monthly 5–10% increases. M-series products reached ~30% of
CCL revenue in September, with management expecting further mix improvement

toward M7/M8 as CSP projects lock in.
Capacity Expansion and M10 Development: Monthly CCL capacity is projected to
expand from 6.18m to 6.48m sheets by year-end. For M9/M10, Nan Ya is
independently formulating hydrocarbon resins and fiberglass incorporating low-
dielectric and quartz materials with solid sampling progress.
TUC (6274 TT)
Financial Highlights:
3Q26 financial results beat market consensus, reporting revenue of NT17.95bn,
GM of 36.9%, operating margin of 31.0%, and EPS of NT$12.88, outperforming

consensus estimates by 29% to 35%.
The earnings beat was driven by continuous growth in high-end product revenue
and a return to profitability for the Thailand plant, which successfully offset earlier
first-half losses.
Operational Outlook:
For 4Q26, high-end product pricing is initiating upward adjustments. With the
Thailand plant adding another 100k sheets of capacity and all series of CCL seeing
price hike opportunities (especially high-end M7/M8 catch-up price increases

alongside tightening mid-to-low-end fiberglass cloth supply), product mix and
profitability are set to improve further.
ASIC AI servers are emerging as a core growth engine, alongside long-term
opportunities from satellite and space data centers. TUC is adding several large
ASIC customers (some already in sampling/board qualification stages) and
expanding its customer base in 400G, 800G, and 1.6T switches.
Key Q&A / Detailed Highlights:
ASIC & Switch Expansion: AI servers will serve as the primary growth driver, with

multiple major ASIC clients entering the board-maker certification stage;
meanwhile, existing 400G switch customers are rapidly accelerating 800G and 1.6T
switch certifications, with some expecting 1.6T demand to outpace 800G by 2027.
Satellite and PTFE Solutions: Space satellite PCBs utilizing M8+ materials are slated
to begin contribution in 1H27, with long-term customer demand forecasts already
extended through 2032. For PTFE, TUC boasts ~5–6 years of R&D foundation

rather than being a newcomer, though external partnerships may be pursued to
bypass special high-temperature press machine capacity limitations
10/30/26, 4:46 PM Taiwan Earnings Call Weekly Recap - Is Earnings Still Enough to Drive Stock Price? | GSTW
https://marquee.gs.com/s/content/markets/2971c821-c2cc-403a-acfe-2b906dd125c4 4/11

### Page 5

Win Semiconductors (3105 TT)
Financial Highlights:
3Q26 revenue reached NT$5.86bn (+11.5% QoQ / +31% YoY) with capacity
utilization improving to 75%. GM expanded significantly by 9.2ppts QoQ to an 18-
quarter high of 37.4% (standalone GM reached 40.7%), while OPM rose to 24.2%
and EPS reached NT$4.55.
Margin expansion was primarily driven by a favorable product mix shift toward
higher-margin Infrastructure and Optical products rather than utilization gains

alone.
Operational Outlook:
Management guided 4Q26 revenue for low-single-digit QoQ growth—beating
normal smartphone seasonality—with gross margins sustaining in the high-30%
range.
Moving into FY27, photodetector (PD) production is expected to ramp materially,
while CW Laser and EML are anticipated to contribute more meaningfully starting
in FY28[cite: 388].
Key Q&A:
Infrastructure and Satellite Growth: Infrastructure became WIN's largest revenue

contributor at 35–40% in 3Q26, driven by LEO satellites, aerospace, and long-
distance communications; demand is expected to stay robust into FY27.
Optical and PD Production Timeline: Optical revenue rose QoQ on initial PD mass
production; PD will see significantly higher full-year shipment volumes in FY27,
while CW Laser and EML will generate limited initial revenue before a FY28 volume
ramp.
